Nanjing (Classic of Difficulties)难经

Chapter 13卷四 論病

Original · plain Chinese · English · notes原文 · 白话 · 英文 · 学习要点

Classical texts医书对照 › Nanjing (Classic of Difficulties)难经 › Chapter 13卷四 論病

四十八難

第四十八难

Difficulty 48

💡 《难经》共八十一难，卷四“论病”从第四十八难起，专论病证的分类与病邪来源。本篇三难：四十八难讲虚实的三种判断法，四十九难讲内伤与外感的辨别（以心病为例），五十难讲五种邪气的名称。三难由浅入深，是理解五行生克如何用于病机的基础。

四十八難曰︰人有三虛三實，何謂也？

问：人有“三虚三实”，指的是什么？

Question: It is said that a person can show three kinds of deficiency and three kinds of excess. What does this mean?

💡 “三虚三实”即脉、病、诊三个层面各有虚实。虚实是八纲（阴阳、表里、寒热、虚实）之一，经文用虚实把判断方法分成三层。这个思路今天仍然适用，考试和临床都要掌握。

然︰有脈之虛實，有病之虛實，有診之虛實也。

答：有脉象上的虚实，有病情上的虚实，有诊察（按触）上的虚实。

Answer: There is deficiency and excess in the pulse, in the disease, and in palpatory examination.

💡 三者分别是脉诊、病证（病因病势与症状）、按诊。今天可对应：脉诊、辨证、腹诊与体表触诊。

脈之虛實者，濡者為虛，緊牢者為實。

脉的虚实：脉濡软的属虚，脉紧而牢（坚实有力）的属实。

Deficiency and excess in the pulse: a soft (ru) pulse indicates deficiency; a tight (jin) or firm (lao) pulse indicates excess.

💡 濡脉：浮而细软，如帛在水中。紧脉：绷紧如转绳，主寒、主痛。牢脉：沉而实大弦长，位置深。这是古代脉名，今天脉学仍沿用，但《难经》此处只是举例。实际诊脉要结合六部和兼脉，不能只凭这两项定虚实。

病之虛實者，出者為虛，入者為實︰言者為虛，不言者為實；緩者為虛，急者為實。

病情的虚实：病由内向外发出的属虚，由外向内侵入的属实；病人爱说话的属虚，不爱说话的属实；病来得缓慢的属虚，病来得急骤的属实。

Deficiency and excess in the disease: that which goes outward is deficiency, that which enters inward is excess; one who talks is deficient, one who stays silent is excess; a slow onset is deficiency, an urgent onset is excess.

💡 这是三组对比：病位走向（出、入）、神志语言（言、不言）、病势缓急。后文注文会逐一解释。这些只是经验性的概括，不是绝对规则。例如久病也可能急性发作，邪实而神志清醒的人也很多，所以要结合整体辨证。

診之虛實者，濡者為虛，牢者為實；癢者為虛，痛者為實。外痛內快，為外實內虛；內痛外快，為內實外虛。故曰虛實也。

按诊的虚实：按之柔软的属虚，按之坚硬的属实；痒属虚，痛属实。表面按之痛而深按反觉舒服的，是表实里虚；深按疼痛而浅按舒服的，是里实表虚。所以叫做虚实。

Deficiency and excess in palpation: where the tissue feels soft it is deficiency, where it feels hard and firm it is excess; itching suggests deficiency, pain suggests excess. Pain on superficial pressure with comfort on deep pressure means exterior excess and interior deficiency; pain on deep pressure with comfort on superficial pressure means interior excess and exterior deficiency. This is what is meant by deficiency and excess.

💡 这是古代按诊（触诊）的原则：喜按、按之舒服属虚，拒按、按之痛属实。今天腹诊和穴位触诊仍用这一原则。例如按期门（LR14）、中脘（CV12）等募穴或腹部压痛点，可辅助判断脏腑虚实。“外痛内快”的具体含义，历代注家说法不一，上述译法是按字面和后世注文的通行理解，学习时不必过度推演。

虛者，空虛，正氣不足也。實者，強實，邪氣有餘也。

虚，是空虚，指正气不足；实，是强实，指邪气有余。

Deficiency means emptiness: the upright qi is insufficient. Excess means solidity: the pathogenic qi is in surplus.

💡 这是对虚实最简明的定义，出自《素问·通评虚实论》“邪气盛则实，精气夺则虚”的思路。今天中医考试的标准定义依然如此。正气约指机体抗病与修复能力，邪气指致病因素。

以脈言之，濡者軟細，故為虛也。緊牢者，緊弦勁，牢沉勁，故為實也。然脈之虛實，不僅乎此，舉此可類推也。

从脉象来说，濡脉软而细，所以属虚。紧脉弦而有劲，牢脉沉而有劲，所以属实。但脉的虚实不只这几种，举这几种是让人可以类推其他。

As for the pulse: the soft pulse is soft and thin, hence deficiency. The tight pulse is taut and forceful, and the firm pulse is deep and forceful, hence excess. However, deficiency and excess in the pulse are not limited to these; these are given as examples from which others can be inferred.

💡 注文提醒：这里只是举例，其他脉如虚脉、细脉、弱脉偏虚，滑脉、洪脉、弦脉多偏实，不是只有濡、紧、牢。可以用“有力无力”作为通用标准：按之有力为实，无力为虚。

以病言之，出者為虛，是五臟自病，由內而之外，所謂內傷是也。入者為實，是五邪所中，由外而之內，所謂外感是也。

从病来说，“出”的属虚，指五脏自己生病，由内向外发展，就是所谓内伤。“入”的属实，指被五种邪气所侵，由外向内发展，就是所谓外感。

As for disease: 'going out' means deficiency, that is, the five zang organs themselves fall ill and the disease spreads from inside to outside; this is what is called internal damage. 'Coming in' means excess, that is, the disease is struck by the five pathogens and spreads from outside to inside; this is what is called external contraction.

💡 注文把“出、入”解释为内伤与外感，这是为了引出下一难（四十九难）。今天可理解为：内伤多为脏腑功能失调、慢性虚损；外感多为急性感染或外邪侵袭。这是简化的大致规律，后面注文自己也说有例外。

然出者間亦有實，入者間亦有虛，此言其大概耳。

不过“出”的偶尔也有实证，“入”的偶尔也有虚证，这里说的只是大概规律。

However, 'going out' can occasionally be excess, and 'coming in' can occasionally be deficiency. This describes only the general tendency.

💡 注文自己承认有例外。学习时要明白，古代讲“大概”的地方很多，如内伤久病也可夹瘀血、痰湿（实），外感后期也会气阴两虚（虚）。不要把这类对比当成硬规则。

言者為虛，以病氣內乏，神氣自清，故惺惺而不妨於言也。不言者為實，以邪氣外攻，入鬱於內，故神志昏亂而不言也。

爱说话的属虚，因为病气是内部不足，神志本身清楚，所以精神清醒，不妨碍说话。不爱说话的属实，因为邪气从外攻入，郁结在内，所以神志昏乱，不能说话。

One who talks is deficient: the disease qi is a lack within, the spirit remains clear, so the person is alert and able to speak. One who is silent is excess: the pathogenic qi attacks from outside and becomes stagnant within, so the spirit is clouded and confused and the person cannot speak.

💡 这是用“神志与语言”来判断虚实。今天的对应：高热、脑炎、中风、谵妄等实证常有意识障碍或失语；某些虚证（如慢性消耗性疾病）患者意识清楚，仍能交谈。但并不是所有虚证都话多，所以这条只是经验性的参考，临床上看到意识障碍要立即按急症处理，不能只用中医虚实去解释。

緩者為虛，以緩病來遲，正氣奪而邪氣微，則病漸深也。急者為實，以急病來驟，正氣漓而邪氣盛，則病疾速也。

病缓的属虚，因为缓病来得迟，是正气被耗夺而邪气不盛，所以病情逐渐加深。病急的属实，因为急病来得猛，是正气浇薄而邪气强盛，所以病情发展迅速。

A slow onset is deficiency: a slow disease arrives late, the upright qi is depleted while the pathogenic qi is slight, so the illness gradually deepens. An urgent onset is excess: an acute disease arrives suddenly, and although the upright qi is thinned, the pathogenic qi is strong, so the illness progresses rapidly.

💡 原文“正气漓”的“漓”指稀薄、耗减，注文这里把急病解释为邪盛。今天可对比：急性感染、急性炎症多属实证；慢性消耗、退行性疾病多属虚证。但慢性病急性发作、虚实夹杂都很常见，临床要分清标本。

診者，按也，候也。按其外而知之，非診脈之診也。

诊，就是按、就是候。按其体表而得知病情，这里的“诊”不是指诊脉。

Examination (zhen) means pressing and observing. One presses the outside to know the condition; this is not the 'examination' of taking the pulse.

💡 这里的“诊”是按诊（触诊），与脉诊区分。今天在针灸临床上，按诊包括体表、经络循行线、背俞穴和募穴、腹部等部位的触诊，用来找压痛、结节、条索、温度变化。

以診候言之，癢者為虛，血氣少而肌肉不充則癢。痛者為實，邪氣聚而營衛不和則痛。又凡虛者喜按，實者拒按，故按之而痛者為實，按之而快者為虛也。

从诊察来说，痒属虚，是因为血气少，肌肉得不到充养才痒。痛属实，是因为邪气聚集，营卫不和才痛。另外，凡是虚证都喜欢按，实证都拒绝按，所以按了痛的属实，按了舒服的属虚。

In terms of examination: itching suggests deficiency, because with little qi and blood the muscles are not nourished and so they itch. Pain suggests excess, because pathogenic qi gathers and the nutritive and defensive qi are out of harmony, so there is pain. Moreover, in deficiency the patient likes pressure, in excess the patient refuses pressure; therefore pain on pressure means excess, and comfort on pressure means deficiency.

💡 “虚者喜按，实者拒按”至今是中医诊断的经典规则，考试必背。例如：腹痛喜按、按之痛减多为虚寒；腹痛拒按、按之痛剧多为积滞或瘀血。现代医学的对照：腹部压痛、反跳痛、肌紧张提示器质性病变（如腹膜炎），要警惕急腹症，这种情况不要仅靠针灸处理。“痒为虚”是古代理论，今天痒的原因很多（过敏、皮肤病、肝胆疾病、尿毒症等），要当作中医的一个思路，不能当成确诊依据。

濡者為虛，牢者為實，《脈經》引用此條，無此二句，或因上文而重出也。楊氏謂按之皮肉柔濡者為虛，牢強者為實，似亦可解，姑存備參。

“濡者为虚，牢者为实”这两句，《脉经》引用这一条时没有，可能是因为上文已经讲过而在这里重复出现。杨氏说这是按之皮肉柔软的属虚、坚强的属实，似乎也讲得通，姑且保留作参考。

The two sentences 'soft means deficiency, firm means excess' are absent when the Maijing (Pulse Classic) quotes this passage; perhaps they are a repetition of the earlier lines. Master Yang takes them to mean that skin and flesh which feel soft on pressure indicate deficiency and those which feel firm and strong indicate excess. This also seems explicable, so it is kept here for reference.

💡 这是版本校勘性质的注文。《脉经》是西晋王叔和的脉学专著。杨氏指唐代杨玄操，他曾为《难经》作注。这段说明古籍流传中存在重复或衍文的可能，学习时要知道古籍有版本问题，但不影响主旨：按之软为虚，按之硬为实。

四十九難

第四十九难

Difficulty 49

💡 本难讲“正经自病”与“五邪所伤”的区别，以心病为例，把五脏、五色、五臭、五味、五声、五液与五行生克结合起来。这是《难经》辨证的典型思维，也是五十难的铺垫。

四十九難曰︰有正經自病，有五邪所傷。何以別之？

问：有的是本经自己生病，有的是被五种邪气所伤。怎么区分？

Question: There is the case where a regular channel falls ill by itself, and the case where one is harmed by the five pathogens. How are they distinguished?

💡 “正经”指本脏本经；“五邪”在后文是风、暑、饮食劳倦、寒、湿。这种分类既有五行系统（五脏）的影子，又包含了外感六淫和内伤饮食劳倦，所以后世注家颇有争论。

然︰憂愁思慮則傷心；形寒飲冷則傷肺；恚怒氣逆，上而不下則傷肝；飲食勞倦則傷脾，久坐濕地，強力入水，則傷腎。是正經之自病也。

答：忧愁思虑就伤心；身体受寒又喝冷饮就伤肺；愤怒使气上逆而不下降就伤肝；饮食不节、劳累疲倦就伤脾；久坐潮湿之地，勉强用力又入水，就伤肾。这些是本经自己生的病。

Answer: Worry, grief and over-thinking harm the heart. Chilled body and cold drinks harm the lung. Rage with qi rebelling upward and not descending harms the liver. Improper eating and overwork harm the spleen. Sitting long on damp ground, forcing oneself to exert and entering water harm the kidney. These are the self-originated diseases of the regular channels.

💡 这一段脱胎于《灵枢·邪气脏腑病形》，把情志、饮食、劳逸、寒湿等病因配属五脏。今天仍可作病因学参考：长期焦虑、劳累、饮食不节确实会导致相应系统失调。但五行配属（思配心，而《素问》则是思伤脾）在不同经典中不一致，考试时要以题目指定的出处为准。

正經，本經也。五邪，五臟之邪也。心主思慮，若憂勞過用，則傷其心。肺主皮毛，形寒者，皮毛外受風寒也；飲冷者，內飲冷水也，其藏本寒，過則傷肺也。肝主怒恚，怒則木氣鬱而傷肝也。

正经，就是本经。五邪，就是五脏的邪气。心主思虑，如果忧愁劳心过度，就伤心。肺主皮毛，“形寒”是皮毛从外受了风寒；“饮冷”是从内喝了冷水，肺脏本来就偏寒，过度就伤肺。肝主怒，发怒使木气郁结而伤肝。

'Regular channel' means the organ's own channel; the 'five pathogens' are the pathogens of the five zang organs. The heart governs thought and worry, so excessive anxiety and overwork harm the heart. The lung governs skin and body hair; 'chilled body' means the skin and hair receive wind-cold from outside, while 'cold drinks' means cold water taken in from within; the organ is inherently cold, so excess harms the lung. The liver governs anger, and anger makes wood qi stagnate and harms the liver.

💡 注文用脏腑功能解释原文。“肺主皮毛”是中医经典理论，对应现代的皮肤与呼吸道的免疫防御关系；“肝主怒、怒伤肝”是情志与肝气郁结（现代可理解为情绪压力引起的自主神经和内分泌紊乱）的关系。注意，注文说“脏本寒”，是古代理论，今天不能照搬。

脾主四肢，勞倦太過則傷脾，脾運五穀，飲食不潔，則亦傷也。腎主骨，用力作強，坐濕入水則傷腎，蓋腎屬水，同氣相感也。然憂思恚怒，飲食動作，人之不能無者，惟不可太過，過則傷人必矣。

脾主四肢，劳倦过度就伤脾；脾运化五谷，饮食不洁也会伤脾。肾主骨，用力过度、坐在潮湿之处或入水就伤肾，因为肾属水，同类之气相互感应。然而忧思、愤怒、饮食、劳动，是人不可能没有的，只是不可太过，过了就一定伤人。

The spleen governs the four limbs, so excessive fatigue harms the spleen; the spleen transports the five grains, so unclean food also harms it. The kidney governs the bones, so over-exertion, sitting in dampness or entering water harms the kidney, since the kidney belongs to water and like qi responds to like. Yet worry, anger, eating and activity are things no person can be without; the only thing is not to go to excess, for excess will surely harm a person.

💡 “同气相感”是五行中“同类相应”的观念：湿寒属水，与肾同类。这段结尾“不可太过”是中医养生的核心：病因不是情志饮食本身，而是“过度”。今天仍然成立，慢性压力、过劳、饮食失节确实是很多慢性病的危险因素。

何謂五邪？

问：什么叫五邪？

Question: What are the five pathogens?

然︰有中風，有傷暑，有飲食勞倦，有傷寒，有中濕。此之謂五邪。

答：有中风，有伤暑，有饮食劳倦，有伤寒，有中湿。这就叫五邪。

Answer: There is wind strike, summer-heat damage, damage from improper diet and overwork, cold damage, and dampness strike. These are called the five pathogens.

💡 “五邪”在这里对应五脏：风—肝，暑—心，饮食劳倦—脾，寒—肺，湿—肾。注意它把“饮食劳倦”也算作一邪，与一般外感六淫不同，所以注文才有后面的争论。学习时要与“正经自病”分开：自病讲本脏，五邪讲他脏之邪入本脏。

肝為風木，故風先入肝。心為君火，暑火之邪，故心受之。飲食勞倦，一味太過，則脾傷致病矣。寒侵皮毛則傷肺。雨霧蒸濕之氣則傷腎。此五者邪由外至，所謂外傷者也。

肝属风木，所以风邪先入肝。心属君火，暑是火热之邪，所以由心受病。饮食劳倦，哪一种太过，就伤脾而致病。寒邪侵犯皮毛就伤肺。雨雾蒸腾的湿气就伤肾。这五种邪气都是从外而来，所以叫外伤。

The liver is wind-wood, so wind enters the liver first. The heart is the sovereign fire, and summer-heat is a fire pathogen, so the heart receives it. With food and overwork, if any single taste or activity is excessive, the spleen is damaged and illness results. Cold invading the skin and body hair harms the lung. The steaming dampness of rain and mist harms the kidney. These five pathogens all arrive from outside; they are what is called external damage.

💡 这是五行配属：风—木—肝，暑—火—心，湿—水（此处配肾）—肾，寒—金（皮毛）—肺，饮食劳倦—土—脾。湿配肾是这一难的特殊说法，后世多把湿配脾，《素问》也不同，所以考试要看清出处。注文把“饮食劳倦”也算作“外伤”，与前文“内伤”讲法有张力，后面“按”的部分会讨论。

按︰《素問‧本病論》、《靈樞‧邪氣臟腑病形篇》與此大同小異。若《素問‧陰陽應象大論》曰︰怒傷肝，喜傷心，思傷脾，憂傷肺，恐傷腎。乃內傷七情，本臟自病之証也。宣明五氣篇曰︰肝惡風，心惡熱，肺惡寒，腎惡燥，脾惡濕。此六淫之邪，外感之証也。皆似同而異。

按：《素问·本病论》和《灵枢·邪气脏腑病形篇》与这里大同小异。而《素问·阴阳应象大论》说：怒伤肝，喜伤心，思伤脾，忧伤肺，恐伤肾，这是内伤七情、本脏自病的病证。《宣明五气篇》说：肝恶风，心恶热，肺恶寒，肾恶燥，脾恶湿，这是六淫之邪、外感的病证。这些看似相同其实有区别。

Note: The Suwen chapter 'Ben Bing Lun' and the Lingshu chapter 'Xie Qi Zang Fu Bing Xing' are broadly the same as this passage, with small differences. By contrast, the Suwen chapter 'Yin Yang Ying Xiang Da Lun' says: anger harms the liver, joy harms the heart, thought harms the spleen, grief harms the lung, fear harms the kidney; these are internal damage by the seven emotions, diseases of the organ itself. The chapter 'Xuan Ming Wu Qi' says: the liver dislikes wind, the heart dislikes heat, the lung dislikes cold, the kidney dislikes dryness, the spleen dislikes dampness; these are the six excesses, patterns of external contraction. They seem alike but differ.

💡 这是对比。《素问》的“怒喜思忧恐”是五志伤五脏，“五恶”是五脏与六气的对应。《难经》这一难把两者混合了。学习提示：考试中“怒伤肝，喜伤心，思伤脾，忧伤肺，恐伤肾”是标准答案，不要和《难经》本难的“忧愁思虑伤心”混淆。六淫在现代仍常用，但要与现代的感染、环境因素对应，不要直接等同。

或謂越人既言本經自病，是從內而生，如形寒飲冷則傷肺，形寒是寒感於皮毛，此從外來也；飲冷，是冷入胸腹，亦從外來也。飲食等亦然。況五邪亦有飲食勞倦，豈非自相矛盾乎？

有人说：秦越人既然说本经自病是从内而生，而像形寒饮冷伤肺，形寒是寒邪感于皮毛，是从外来；饮冷是冷物进入胸腹，也是从外来。饮食等也是这样。何况五邪里也有饮食劳倦，岂不是自相矛盾吗？

Some say: Qin Yueren states that a regular channel's self-originated disease arises from within; yet in 'chilled body and cold drinks harm the lung', the chill is cold contracted at the skin and hair, which comes from outside, and the cold drink enters the chest and abdomen, which also comes from outside. The same applies to food and so on. Moreover, among the five pathogens there is also improper diet and overwork. Is this not self-contradictory?

💡 越人即秦越人（扁鹊），传统上认为是《难经》作者。这是历代对本难的著名质疑：同一病因既算内伤又算外感。这种疑问提示，内伤与外感的界限在古人那里不绝对，要看“正气是否先虚”。

然其意謂正經虛，則不任寒冷之侵伐，侵伐則每易致病。正經虛，又傷於飲食者，為內傷，若傷飲食而致病者，則外感也。

但经文的意思是说，本经本来就虚，就承受不了寒冷的侵伐，一受侵伐就容易生病。本经已虚，又被饮食所伤的，属内伤；如果是单纯因为饮食所伤而致病（本经不虚），则属外感。

However, the meaning is that when the regular channel is already deficient, it cannot withstand the assault of cold, and such assault readily causes illness. When the regular channel is deficient and is further damaged by food and drink, that is internal damage; but if illness arises simply from damage by food and drink, that is external contraction.

💡 这是调和矛盾的解释：同一个因素，在本脏先虚的人身上就是内伤，在本脏不虚的人身上就是外感（外来因素）。这对应今天的“易感体质”：同样的寒冷刺激，免疫力或体质不同的人，反应不同。对学生来说，这提示辨证时要同时考虑邪气和正气两个因素。

《素問》言腎惡燥者，言其水臟而惡燥氣之耗竭也。此云水濕傷腎者，濕傷於下、故濕先歸腎，腎屬水臟，同氣相求也。

《素问》说肾恶燥，是因为肾是水脏，怕燥气把水耗竭。这里说水湿伤肾，是因为湿邪伤人偏于下部，所以湿先归于肾，肾属水脏，同类之气相互吸引。

When the Suwen says the kidney dislikes dryness, it means that as a water organ it dislikes the exhausting effect of dry qi. When this text says that water-dampness harms the kidney, it is because dampness injures the lower body, so dampness goes first to the kidney; the kidney is a water organ and like qi seeks like.

💡 这里调和《素问》的“肾恶燥”与《难经》的“湿伤肾”：前者讲脏本身的特性（水脏怕被耗竭），后者讲湿邪的倾向（湿性趋下、同气相求）。“湿性重浊，易袭阴位”是中医外感病因的经典理论，今天仍用。

是古聖先賢之義，雖有異同，而辨內傷外感之理則一。讀書貴乎融貫，不可執泥，先儒所謂以意逆志，是謂得之，信夫。

这是古代圣贤的意思，虽然有异同，但辨别内伤外感的道理是一致的。读书贵在融会贯通，不可拘泥，前辈儒者所谓“以意逆志”，就是掌握了精髓，确实如此。

The meanings of the ancient sages and worthies may differ in detail, yet the principle of distinguishing internal damage from external contraction is one. In reading, what matters is to integrate and see the whole, not to cling rigidly; what the earlier Confucians called 'using one's understanding to meet the author's intention' is exactly this. How true.

💡 “以意逆志”出自《孟子·万章上》，指读经典要体会作者本意，不要死抠字句。这是对学生的重要提醒：古籍各篇说法不同，要从整体思想理解。

假令心病，何以知中風得之？

问：假如心脏有病，怎么知道是中了风邪而得的？

Question: Suppose the heart is diseased; how does one know it was contracted through wind strike?

💡 接下来以“心病”为例，逐一讲五种邪气入心的表现。这是《难经》常用的“举一反三”方式：以心为例，其他脏照此类推。

然︰其色當赤。何以言之？肝主色，自入為青，入心為赤，入脾為黃，入肺為白，入腎為黑。肝為心邪，故知當赤色也。其病身熱，脅下滿痛，其脈浮大而弦。

答：他的面色应该发红。为什么这样说？肝主色，肝邪自入本脏则见青色，入心则见赤色，入脾则见黄色，入肺则见白色，入肾则见黑色。这里肝邪入心，所以知道面色应当发红。他的症状是身体发热，胁下胀满疼痛，脉浮大而弦。

Answer: The complexion should be red. Why is this said? The liver governs color: when it enters its own organ the color is blue-green, entering the heart it is red, entering the spleen yellow, entering the lung white, entering the kidney black. Here the liver has become a pathogen to the heart, so we know the complexion should be red. The illness shows body heat, fullness and pain below the ribs, and a floating, large and wiry pulse.

💡 五色：肝青、心赤、脾黄、肺白、肾黑。“肝邪入心”在五行上是木生火，母邪传子，后来在五十难叫“虚邪”（从后来）。这段是《难经》的典型方法：以“色”判断邪从哪脏来，以“脉”再验证（心脉浮大，肝脉弦，所以浮大而弦）。胁下满痛可取期门（LR14）、章门（LR13）周围按诊。现代临床上，发热、胁下胀痛要考虑肝胆、胃肠及胸膜疾病，需鉴别，不能只用五行分类。

假令心病者，舉心臟為例也。此言心病，肝邪入而得中風之病，蓋風氣通於肝也。肝開竅於目，故主色，風邪自入肝經，則色青，肝在色為蒼也。入心則色赤，心在色為赤也。入脾則色黃，脾在色為黃也。入肺則色白，肺在色為白也。入腎則色黑，腎在色為黑也。故肝之風邪入心，其色當赤也。其病身熱者，外感之邪，先傷營衛，故身熱，而又心屬火，熱為火邪之象也。

“假令心病”是以心脏举例。这里说心病，是肝邪入心而得中风之病，因为风气与肝相通。肝开窍于目，所以主色，风邪自入肝经则见青色，因为肝在五色中是苍色。入心则见赤色，因为心在色为赤。入脾则见黄色，因为脾在色为黄。入肺则见白色，因为肺在色为白。入肾则见黑色，因为肾在色为黑。所以肝的风邪入心，面色应当发红。他身体发热，是因为外感之邪先伤营卫，所以身热，而且心属火，热是火邪的表现。

'Suppose the heart is diseased' takes the heart as an example. Here, in heart disease, the liver pathogen enters and a wind-strike illness results, since wind qi connects with the liver. The liver opens at the eyes and therefore governs color: when wind pathogen enters the liver channel itself the color is blue-green, since the liver is dark green in color. Entering the heart it is red, since the heart is red; entering the spleen it is yellow; entering the lung white; entering the kidney black. Therefore wind pathogen of the liver entering the heart makes the complexion red. The body heat arises because an externally contracted pathogen first harms the nutritive and defensive qi, hence fever; moreover the heart belongs to fire, and heat is the manifestation of fire pathogen.

💡 这段重复并补充了五色配属。“肝开窍于目”在今天仍是中医理论（目与肝的关系，如肝火上炎见目赤）。“营卫”指营气与卫气，外感初期卫气与邪相争则发热，这是《伤寒论》常见思路。把“风”直接对应“肝”是五行配属，现代不一定如此对应，临床要看实际症状。

脅下滿痛者，脅下，肝之位也。其脈浮大而弦者，浮大心脈本象，肝邪犯之，故現弦脈也。

胁下胀满疼痛，是因为胁下是肝所在的部位。脉浮大而弦，浮大是心脉本来的脉象，肝邪侵犯它，所以出现弦脉。

Fullness and pain below the ribs occur because the area below the ribs is the location of the liver. A floating, large and wiry pulse: floating and large is the heart's own pulse image; the liver pathogen assails it, so a wiry pulse appears.

💡 原则是：本脏脉象＋邪气所属脏的脉象，这是《难经》十难的“一脉十变”思路。五脏脉：心浮大而散，肝弦，脾缓，肺浮涩而短，肾沉濡而滑，这是本篇后面反复用到的基础。肝经循行胁肋，所以胁下痛可取肝经穴位如太冲（LR3）。

何以知傷暑得之？

问：怎么知道是伤暑而得的？

Question: How does one know it was contracted through summer-heat damage?

然︰當惡臭。何以言之？心主臭，自入為焦臭，入脾為香臭，入肝為臊臭，入腎為腐臭，入肺為腥臭。故知心病傷暑得之，當惡臭。其病身熱而煩，心痛，其脈浮大而散。

答：应当有难闻的臭气。为什么这样说？心主臭，心邪自入本脏为焦臭，入脾为香臭，入肝为臊臭，入肾为腐臭，入肺为腥臭。所以知道心病因伤暑而得的，应当有恶臭。他的症状是身热而心烦，心痛，脉浮大而散。

Answer: There should be a foul smell. Why is this said? The heart governs odor: when it enters its own organ the odor is scorched, entering the spleen it is fragrant, entering the liver it is rank, entering the kidney it is putrid, entering the lung it is fishy. So we know that heart disease from summer-heat damage should have a foul odor. The illness shows body heat with vexation, heart pain, and a floating, large and scattered pulse.

💡 五臭：心焦、脾香、肝臊、肾腐、肺腥（《素问·金匮真言论》也有类似说法）。“恶臭”另有一说读作“厌恶气味”（恶音wù），与后文“喜苦味”相对；本书注文作“发恶臭”，译文从之。“伤暑”是心自病，没有他脏之邪介入，所以称正邪。现代对应：中暑、热射病可出现高热、烦躁、胸闷、心悸等症状。“心痛”在古代范围很广，包括心绞痛、胃脘痛等；今天如果出现压榨性胸痛、冷汗、呼吸困难，必须按急症（如心肌梗死）处理，立即就医。针灸学习上可记内关（PC6）、神门（HT7）作为心胸常用穴位，但急症不能延误。“散脉”是浮而无根，散乱无力。

假令心病而傷暑，暑之傷人，心先得之，蓋心主暑也。此正經自病，不涉他經，然心屬火，暑熱之邪傷之，火邪化物，五臭出焉，暑邪自入本經，其臭焦，火之氣也。入脾其臭香，土之氣也。

假如心病而伤暑，暑邪伤人，心最先受病，因为心主暑。这是本经自病，不涉及其他经，然而心属火，暑热之邪伤了它，火邪化生万物，五种气味由此产生。暑邪进入本经，气味焦，这是火之气。入脾，气味香，这是土之气。

Suppose the heart is diseased through summer-heat damage. When summer-heat harms a person, the heart receives it first, since the heart governs summer-heat. This is self-originated disease of the regular channel and does not involve other channels; yet the heart belongs to fire, and when heat pathogen harms it, fire transforms things and the five odors emerge. When summer-heat pathogen enters its own channel the odor is scorched, which is the qi of fire. Entering the spleen the odor is fragrant, which is the qi of earth.

💡 “心主暑”是《难经》的说法，暑对应火、夏、心。注文强调伤暑是心的“正经自病”，与前面讲的“五邪由外至”有概念交叉，这又是本难的一个难点：暑邪从外来，但以本脏受之，所以称正邪（自病）。学习时记住：五十难的“正邪”就是指这种。

入肝其臭臊，木之氣也。入腎其臭腐，水之氣也。入肺其臭腥，金之氣也。故心受暑邪，發惡臭也。

入肝，气味臊，这是木之气。入肾，气味腐，这是水之气。入肺，气味腥，这是金之气。所以心受暑邪，会发出恶臭。

Entering the liver the odor is rank, which is the qi of wood. Entering the kidney the odor is putrid, which is the qi of water. Entering the lung the odor is fishy, which is the qi of metal. Therefore when the heart receives summer-heat pathogen, a foul odor emerges.

💡 这里把五臭和五行对应起来：木臊、火焦、土香、金腥、水腐。这是古代理论，今天不可用来单独诊断。但临床上口气、体味、分泌物气味（如酮症酸中毒的烂苹果味、尿毒症的氨味、肝性口臭）确实是有价值的线索，这是“闻诊”的实用部分。

其病身熱而煩者，火鬱則瞀亂也。心痛者，邪在心則痛也。其脈浮大而散者，浮大心之本脈，散則浮大而空虛無神，心之病脈也。本臟自病，心主臭，故專以臭推也。

身热而烦，是因为火邪郁结则神志昏乱。心痛，是因为邪在心就疼痛。脉浮大而散，浮大是心的本脉，散就是浮大而空虚、没有神气，是心的病脉。本脏自己生病，心主臭，所以专门用气味来推断。

Body heat with vexation arises because fire stagnating causes mental confusion. Heart pain arises because pathogen in the heart causes pain. A floating, large and scattered pulse: floating and large is the heart's own pulse, while scattered means floating and large but empty and spiritless, the heart's diseased pulse. The organ itself is ill, and since the heart governs odor, the inference is made specifically from odor.

💡 “神”在脉学中指有胃气、有根、有力。散脉无神，是气血耗散的征象，现代临床上可见于严重心衰、心律不整等危重情况，需要重视。“瞀乱”指视物昏花、神志烦乱。对照现代，热病导致心烦意乱常见于高热、中暑、甲亢等。

何以知飲食勞倦得之？

问：怎么知道是饮食劳倦而得的？

Question: How does one know it was contracted through improper diet and overwork?

然︰當喜苦味也。虛為不欲食，實為欲食。何以言之？脾主味，入肝為酸，入心為苦，入肺為辛，入腎為鹹，自入為甘。故知脾邪入心，為喜苦味也。其病身熱而體重嗜臥，四肢不收，其脈浮大而緩。

答：应当喜欢苦味。虚证不想吃东西，实证想吃东西。为什么这样说？脾主味，脾邪入肝则味酸，入心则味苦，入肺则味辛，入肾则味咸，自入本脏则味甘。所以知道是脾邪入心，会喜欢苦味。他的症状是身热而身体沉重、嗜睡，四肢松软不能自主，脉浮大而缓。

Answer: The person should favor bitter taste. Deficiency means no desire to eat; excess means desire to eat. Why is this said? The spleen governs taste: when its pathogen enters the liver the taste is sour, entering the heart bitter, entering the lung acrid, entering the kidney salty, and entering its own organ sweet. So we know that spleen pathogen entering the heart makes the person favor bitter taste. The illness shows body heat, heaviness of the body and sleepiness, limpness of the four limbs, and a floating, large and slow-relaxed pulse.

💡 脾邪入心是土之邪入火，火生土，所以是“子邪”（后来称实邪）。症状以身热（心）加体重嗜卧、四肢不收（脾）为特点，脉浮大（心）而缓（脾）。这与现代的疲劳综合征、脂肪肝、代谢综合征表现的倦怠、嗜睡、肢体沉重有相似之处，但不能等同。“虚为不欲食，实为欲食”指脾邪的虚实，后面注文会解释。针灸可考虑足三里（ST36）、三阴交（SP6）调脾胃，内关（PC6）、神门（HT7）调心神。

假令心病而傷飲食勞倦者，心主熱，脾主勞倦，今心病以飲食勞倦得之，故知脾邪入心也。喜苦味者，脾主味，心屬火，火味苦，從其性也。虛則脾氣不能化穀，實則能化穀，故有能食不能食之分也。

假如心病是因为饮食劳倦而得的，心主热，脾主劳倦，现在心病是由饮食劳倦所致，所以知道是脾邪入心。喜欢苦味，是因为脾主味，心属火，火的味道是苦，顺从了它的本性。虚的时候脾气不能消化谷物，实的时候能消化，所以有能吃和不能吃的区别。

Suppose the heart is diseased through improper diet and overwork. The heart governs heat and the spleen governs fatigue; now heart disease is contracted through diet and overwork, so we know spleen pathogen has entered the heart. Favoring bitter taste: the spleen governs taste, the heart belongs to fire, and the taste of fire is bitter; it follows its nature. In deficiency the spleen qi cannot transform grain; in excess it can, hence the distinction between being able and unable to eat.

💡 “喜苦味”是古代的味觉偏嗜理论。今天可作参考：偏嗜某种口味有时与生理状态有关（如孕期、锌缺乏），但不能用来诊断。“能食不能食”是分辨脾邪虚实的线索：不欲食多为脾虚，能食多为胃火或实热。这是临床上常用的线索，如糖尿病的“多食易饥”（中消）。

若肝受飲食勞倦之病，其味酸；心受病，其味苦，肺受病，其味辛；腎受病，其味鹹；脾自受病，其味甘。

如果肝受了饮食劳倦的病，味酸；心受病，味苦；肺受病，味辛；肾受病，味咸；脾自己受病，味甘。

If the liver receives the illness of improper diet and overwork, the taste is sour; if the heart receives it, the taste is bitter; the lung, acrid; the kidney, salty; and if the spleen itself receives the disease, the taste is sweet.

💡 这里把五味与五脏列出：酸—肝，苦—心，辛—肺，咸—肾，甘—脾。这是《素问》中五味入五脏的经典对应，今天用于药性、食疗的归经参考，但不能直接用来诊断。

其病身熱者，心也。體重，脾也。其脈浮大者，心之本脈也；緩，脾之脈象也。此節飲食勞倦，獨有虛實之分者，蓋即明正經虛，又傷於飲食而為病，較傷飲食而致病者有間也。

身热，是心的表现。身体沉重，是脾的表现。脉浮大，是心的本脉；缓，是脾的脉象。这一节饮食劳倦，单独有虚实之分，是为了说明本经已虚、又伤于饮食而生病的情况，与单纯伤于饮食而致病的情况有区别。

Body heat is the heart. Heaviness of the body is the spleen. The floating, large pulse is the heart's own pulse; relaxed-slow is the spleen's pulse image. In this section, only the diet-and-overwork category has the distinction between deficiency and excess; this is to clarify that when the regular channel is already deficient and is further damaged by food, the disease differs from that caused by food damage alone.

💡 这是把前面“正经虚又伤于饮食为内伤，单纯伤饮食为外感”的观点再次落实：同一个病因，虚实性质不同，表现也不同。这对临床的启示是：同一种诱因（如暴饮暴食），在体质虚弱和体质强壮的人身上，证型不同，治疗也不同。

何以知傷寒得之？

问：怎么知道是伤寒而得的？

Question: How does one know it was contracted through cold damage?

然︰當譫言妄語。何以言之？肺主聲，入肝為呼，入心為言，入脾為歌，入腎為呻，自入為哭。故知肺邪入心，為譫言妄語也。其病身熱，洒洒惡寒，甚則喘咳，其脈浮大而澀。

答：应当有谵语妄语。为什么这样说？肺主声，肺邪入肝则发出呼叫声，入心则多言，入脾则歌唱，入肾则呻吟，自入本脏则哭泣。所以知道是肺邪入心，表现为谵语妄语。他的症状是身热，一阵阵发冷怕寒，严重的会喘咳，脉浮大而涩。

Answer: There should be delirious and incoherent speech. Why is this said? The lung governs sound: when its pathogen enters the liver there is shouting, entering the heart there is speech, entering the spleen singing, entering the kidney groaning, and entering its own organ weeping. So we know lung pathogen has entered the heart, producing delirious and incoherent speech. The illness shows body heat, shivering aversion to cold, and in severe cases panting and cough, with a floating, large and rough pulse.

💡 五声：呼（肝）、笑（心）、歌（脾）、哭（肺）、呻（肾）。这里“入心为言”是另一种说法，注文解释为多言或笑。肺邪入心，是金邪伤火，所谓“微邪”（所胜来）。症状有身热（心）加恶寒、喘咳（肺），脉浮大（心）而涩（肺）。现代对照：高热伴意识障碍、谵妄、呼吸道症状，要考虑重症肺炎、败血症、中枢神经感染，需要及时就医。“谵言妄语”是古代对神志异常的描述，今天要用精神与神经学方法鉴别。

假令心病而傷寒者，乃肺邪入心也。肺主聲，故譫言妄語也。若寒邪入肝則呼，肝在聲為呼也。

假如心病而伤寒，就是肺邪入心。肺主声，所以谵言妄语。如果寒邪入肝就呼叫，因为肝在声为呼。

Suppose the heart is diseased through cold damage; this is lung pathogen entering the heart. The lung governs sound, hence delirious and incoherent speech. If cold pathogen enters the liver there is shouting, since the liver corresponds to shouting among the sounds.

💡 这里开始逐个解释五声。注意《难经》把寒邪和肺相配（肺主皮毛、寒伤皮毛），但这里又说“肺邪入心”，指的是以肺为邪源入心。

入心則多言，言為心聲，又在聲為笑也。入脾則歌，脾在聲為歌也。入腎則呻。腎在聲為呻也，自入肺之本臟則哭，肺在聲為哭也。

入心则多言，因为言语是心的声音，而且心在声为笑。入脾则歌唱，因为脾在声为歌。入肾则呻吟，因为肾在声为呻。自入肺的本脏则哭泣，因为肺在声为哭。

Entering the heart there is much speech, since speech is the voice of the heart, and the heart also corresponds to laughter among the sounds. Entering the spleen there is singing, since the spleen corresponds to singing. Entering the kidney there is groaning, since the kidney corresponds to groaning. Entering its own organ, the lung, there is weeping, since the lung corresponds to weeping.

💡 五声对应：肝呼、心笑（或言）、脾歌、肺哭、肾呻（《素问》作“呼笑歌哭呻”）。今天的闻诊仍然关注声音变化，如声哑、呻吟、谵语、语言错乱，但要结合现代神经精神与呼吸系统的检查。

其病身熱惡寒者，心，火臟，故身熱，肺本寒臟，故惡寒也。甚則喘咳者，肺主咳，肺氣上逆，則喘咳也。其脈浮大，心脈也，澀，肺之脈象也。

身热恶寒，是因为心是火脏，所以身热；肺本来是寒脏，所以恶寒。严重的会喘咳，是因为肺主咳，肺气上逆就喘咳。脉浮大是心脉，涩是肺的脉象。

Body heat with aversion to cold: the heart is a fire organ, hence body heat; the lung is inherently a cold organ, hence aversion to cold. In severe cases, panting and cough: the lung governs cough, and when lung qi rebels upward there is panting and cough. The floating, large pulse is the heart's pulse; the rough pulse is the lung's pulse image.

💡 “肺气上逆则喘咳”是中医经典理论，肺气以宣发肃降为顺。今天可对应气道疾病（支气管炎、哮喘、COPD、肺炎）。“恶寒身热”在外感初期常见，今天可能是流感或其他呼吸道感染的表现。针灸上可考虑肺经穴位如列缺（LU7）、尺泽（LU5），但重症仍需西医处理。

何以知中濕得之？

问：怎么知道是中湿而得的？

Question: How does one know it was contracted through dampness strike?

然︰當喜汗出不可止。何以言之？腎主液，入肝為泣，入心為汗，入脾為涎，入肺為涕，自入為唾。故知腎邪入心，為汗出不可止也。其病身熱而小腹痛，足脛寒而逆，其脈沉濡而大。此五邪之法也。

答：应当多汗而不止。为什么这样说？肾主液，肾邪入肝为泪，入心为汗，入脾为涎，入肺为涕，自入为唾。所以知道是肾邪入心，表现为汗出不止。他的症状是身热而小腹疼痛，足胫寒冷而逆冷，脉沉濡而大。这就是五邪的辨法。

Answer: The person should have sweating that cannot be stopped. Why is this said? The kidney governs fluids: its pathogen entering the liver makes tears, entering the heart sweat, entering the spleen drool, entering the lung nasal mucus, and entering its own organ spittle. So we know kidney pathogen has entered the heart, producing uncontrollable sweating. The illness shows body heat with lower abdominal pain, cold and reversed-cold feet and shins, and a deep, soft and large pulse. This is the method for the five pathogens.

💡 五液：肝泪、心汗、脾涎、肺涕、肾唾。肾邪入心是水克火，所以称“贼邪”（所不胜来），预后较重。症状有身热（心）、小腹痛、足胫寒逆（肾），脉沉濡（肾）而大（心）。针灸可考虑调节出汗的穴位：复溜（KI7）配合谷（LI4），这是古代以来的经典配方，常用于汗出不止或无汗。小腹痛可取关元（CV4）、中极（CV3）。如果是现代的急腹症、泌尿生殖系统感染等，要及时就医。

假令心病而中濕者，心主暑，腎主濕，今心病以傷濕得之，故知腎邪入心也。腎化五液，腎為心邪，故汗出不可止也。濕邪入肝為泣，肝主泣也。入心為汗，心主汗也。入脾為涎，脾主涎也。入肺為涕，肺主涕也。自入腎之本臟，則為唾，腎主唾也。其病身熱者，心也。小腹痛者，腎之位也。；足脛寒而逆者，足脛腎經所過之處，故畏寒而逆冷，濕性亦近寒也。其脈沉濡而大者，沉，腎脈之象，濡，濕氣之候，大則心脈之象也。心脈浮大，獨不言浮者，沉則不能浮也。

假如心病是因为中湿而得的，心主暑，肾主湿，现在心病是由湿所伤，所以知道是肾邪入心。肾化生五液，肾成了心的邪，所以汗出不止。湿邪入肝为泪，因为肝主泪。入心为汗，因为心主汗。入脾为涎，因为脾主涎。入肺为涕，因为肺主涕。自入肾的本脏则为唾，因为肾主唾。他身热，是心的表现。小腹痛，是肾所在的部位。足胫寒冷逆冷，是因为足胫是肾经所经过的地方，所以怕冷而逆冷，湿的性质也接近寒。脉沉濡而大，沉是肾脉的象，濡是湿气的征候，大是心脉的象。心脉本来浮大，这里单单没有说浮，是因为脉沉就不能浮了。

Suppose the heart is diseased through dampness strike. The heart governs summer-heat and the kidney governs dampness; now the heart disease is contracted through dampness damage, so we know kidney pathogen has entered the heart. The kidney transforms the five fluids; the kidney has become a pathogen to the heart, hence the sweating that cannot be stopped. Dampness pathogen entering the liver makes tears, as the liver governs tears; entering the heart makes sweat, as the heart governs sweat; entering the spleen makes drool, as the spleen governs drool; entering the lung makes nasal mucus, as the lung governs nasal mucus; entering the kidney's own organ it becomes spittle, as the kidney governs spittle. Body heat is the heart. Lower abdominal pain is the kidney's location. Cold and reversed-cold feet and shins: the shins are where the kidney channel passes, hence aversion to cold and reversed cold, and the nature of dampness is also close to cold. The deep, soft and large pulse: deep is the kidney pulse image, soft is the sign of dampness qi, and large is the heart pulse image. The heart pulse is floating and large; floating is not mentioned alone because when the pulse is deep it cannot float.

💡 肾经从足底涌泉（KI1）起，循足内踝后、小腿内侧上行，所以足胫寒逆与肾经有关，可取太溪（KI3）、复溜（KI7）。湿邪与寒近，这也是中医“寒湿”合称的由来。“沉不能浮”说明脉象中有时要放弃表面矛盾，着眼于整体：脉沉而兼大，说明是肾脉与心脉的混合。

夫法者，舉一為例之法也。五邪者，五臟得五行之邪也。欲知五邪之証，必審肝病見於色，心病見於臭，脾病見於味，肺病見於聲，腎病見於液。其脈以本臟之脈為主，而兼受邪之脈也。此以心一經為主病，而以各証驗其所從來，其義與十難診脈法同。明乎此，不特五臟互受五邪，鑿然可曉。即百病見証，莫不皆可類測，而為診脈辨証之法程也。

所谓“法”，是举一个例子作为方法。五邪，是五脏得到五行之邪。要知道五邪的证候，必须明察：肝病表现在色，心病表现在臭，脾病表现在味，肺病表现在声，肾病表现在液。脉以本脏之脉为主，并兼有所受之邪的脉。这里以心一经作为主病，用各种证候验证邪从何来，其意义与十难诊脉法相同。明白了这个，不但五脏互相受五邪的道理清楚可知，连各种疾病的表现，也都可以类推，成为诊脉辨证的准则。

The 'method' here is the method of taking one example as a model. The five pathogens are the pathogens of the five phases received by the five zang organs. To know the manifestations of the five pathogens one must examine: liver disease appears in color, heart disease in odor, spleen disease in taste, lung disease in sound, and kidney disease in fluid. The pulse takes the pulse of the organ itself as primary, combined with the pulse of the pathogen received. Here the heart channel is taken as the main diseased organ, and the various manifestations are used to verify where the pathogen comes from; the meaning is the same as the pulse method in Difficulty 10. Once this is understood, not only is the mutual reception of the five pathogens by the five zang organs clearly understood, but also the manifestations of all diseases can be inferred by analogy, and this becomes the standard for taking the pulse and differentiating patterns.

💡 这段是整难的总结：色（肝）、臭（心）、味（脾）、声（肺）、液（肾），五种线索分别用来判断邪从哪脏来。脉象是本脏之脉加邪脏之脉。总结要点：以心病为例，五种病邪分别为肝邪（中风，色赤）、心自病（伤暑，恶臭）、脾邪（饮食劳倦，喜苦味）、肺邪（伤寒，谵语）、肾邪（中湿，汗出不止）。这种分析框架至今仍是中医考试的重点，但临床上不能机械套用，要结合其他信息。

五十難

第五十难

Difficulty 50

💡 承接上一难，这一难把“五邪”重新按与病脏的五行生克关系命名：虚邪、实邪、贼邪、微邪、正邪。这个分类在后面的治法（如六十九难的“虚者补其母，实者泻其子”）中很重要。

五十難曰︰病有虛邪，有實邪，有賊邪，有微邪，有正邪，何以別之？

问：病有虚邪，有实邪，有贼邪，有微邪，有正邪，怎么区别？

Question: Disease may involve a deficiency pathogen, an excess pathogen, a thief pathogen, a slight pathogen, or a regular pathogen. How are they distinguished?

💡 这里的“虚邪、实邪”是《难经》中专门的五行关系术语，与《素问》中“虚邪贼风”（指不正常气候）的“虚邪”不是同一意思，考试时要区分。

然︰從後來者為虛邪，從前來者為實邪，從所不勝來者為賊邪，從所勝來者為微邪，自病者為正邪。

答：从“后”来的是虚邪，从“前”来的是实邪，从所不能胜（克我）的那一脏来的是贼邪，从所能胜（我克）的那一脏来的是微邪，本脏自己生病的是正邪。

Answer: That which comes from behind is a deficiency pathogen; that which comes from in front is an excess pathogen; that which comes from what one cannot overcome is a thief pathogen; that which comes from what one overcomes is a slight pathogen; and self-originated disease is a regular pathogen.

💡 以心（火）为例：“后”指生我者（母），即肝木来邪为虚邪（对应前面的中风）；“前”指我生者（子），即脾土来邪为实邪（饮食劳倦）；所不胜（克我者）是肾水，为贼邪（中湿）；所胜（我克者）是肺金，为微邪（伤寒）；本脏自病是正邪（伤暑）。贼邪最重，因为克制本脏；微邪最轻，因为本脏能制它。这个分类可以直接指导治法：“虚则补其母，实则泻其子”（六十九难）。例如心经：补母取少冲（HT9，木穴），泻子取神门（HT7，土穴）。现代临床不一定严格按五行选穴，但这是针灸传统选穴思路之一，考试常考。

此承上文五臟五邪之病，而辨其生克之義也。病有虛邪者，如心臟屬火，其病邪從肝木傳來，木生火，則木位居火之後，是生我者，邪挾生氣而來，雖進而易退，故曰從後來者虛邪也。病有實邪者，如心屬火，其病邪從脾土傳來，火生土，則土位居火之前，是受我之氣者，其力方旺，還而相克，其勢必盛，故從前來者實邪也。病有賊邪者，如心屬火，其病邪從腎水傳來，水克火，心受克而不能勝，臟氣本已相製，而邪氣挾其力而來，殘削必甚，故曰從所不勝來者，賊邪也。病有微邪者，如心屬火，其邪從肺金傳來，火克金，金受克而火能勝，臟氣既受製於我，則邪氣亦不能深入，故曰從所勝來者，微邪也。正邪者，如心臟止有自感之邪，而無他臟干克之邪者是也。

本段承接上文所讲的五脏五种邪气致病，进一步辨析五行生克关系与疾病传变的意义。所谓虚邪，例如心属火，病邪由肝木传来，木生火，肝木在心火的五行生化关系中居于其后方，肝木是生养心火的脏。因此，邪气伴随着生助之气而来，虽然会传入，但容易退去，所以称从后方传来的邪气为虚邪。所谓实邪，例如心属火，病邪由脾土传来，火生土，脾土在心火的生化关系中居于其前方，是接受心火所生之气的脏。脾土此时力量正旺，病邪反过来形成相克之势，因而势头较盛，所以称从前方传来的邪气为实邪。所谓贼邪，例如心属火，病邪由肾水传来，水克火，心受到克制而不能抵抗。脏气原本已经存在五行制约关系，邪气又借助相克之势而来，损伤必然严重，因此称从自身所不能胜过的脏传来的邪气为贼邪。所谓微邪，例如心属火，邪气由肺金传来，火克金，金受到火的克制，心火能够胜过肺金。肺金既然受到心火的制约，邪气也就难以深入，所以称从自身能够克制的一方传来的邪气为微邪。所谓正邪，是指某脏自身感受病邪，并没有其他脏腑传来的干扰或克制之邪。

This passage continues the discussion of the five types of pathogenic influences affecting the five zang organs and explains their relationships through the Five Phases of generation and restriction. A deficiency pathogen, or xu xie, is illustrated by a heart disorder receiving pathogenic influence from the liver. The liver belongs to Wood, which generates Fire, the phase of the heart. The pathogen therefore arrives together with a generating influence. Although it may enter, it is relatively easy to resolve. Hence, a pathogen transmitted from the generating side is called a deficiency pathogen. An excess pathogen, or shi xie, occurs when a heart disorder receives pathogenic influence from the spleen. The spleen belongs to Earth, which is generated by Fire. In this relationship, Earth receives the influence of Fire and is positioned on the receiving side. Its influence is vigorous and may turn into a restrictive force, making the pathogen more intense. Hence, transmission from this side is called an excess pathogen. A thief pathogen, or zei xie, occurs when a heart disorder receives pathogenic influence from the kidney. Kidney Water restricts Heart Fire, and the heart cannot overcome this restriction. The pathogenic influence exploits an already existing restraining relationship and causes substantial damage. It is therefore called a thief pathogen transmitted from the phase that the affected organ cannot overcome. A slight pathogen, or wei xie, occurs when a heart disorder receives pathogenic influence from the lung. Lung Metal is restricted by Heart Fire, which is able to overcome it. The pathogen therefore cannot penetrate deeply, and transmission from the phase that the affected organ can overcome is called a slight pathogen. A regular pathogen, zheng xie, refers to a disorder arising from an organ's own exposure to pathogenic influence, without interference or restriction transmitted from another organ.

💡 核心概念是五邪传变：虚邪、实邪、贼邪、微邪、正邪。必须从受病脏的五行属性出发，判断邪气来自何脏，再分析相生、相克关系。以心火为例：肝木生心火，为虚邪；脾土为心火所生，为实邪；肾水克心火，为贼邪；心火克肺金，为微邪；心自身受邪，为正邪。这里的前后是五行传变关系中的位置，不是身体前后部位，也不应简单理解为时间先后。古代五行模型是传统医学的理论分类体系，并非现代医学证实的器官间能量传递机制。现代临床可将其作为理解古代病机、病势轻重和疾病传变思路的历史框架，不能据此判断实际器官损伤程度。考试重点：准确区分四种相对传变关系及正邪的定义。

何以言之？假令心病，中風得之為虛邪，傷暑得之為正邪，飲食勞倦得之為實邪，傷寒得之為微邪，中濕得之為賊邪。

为什么这样说呢？假设发生心脏方面的疾病，如果是因感受风邪而得病，属于虚邪；如果是因感受暑邪而得病，属于正邪；如果是因饮食不节、劳累过度而得病，属于实邪；如果是因感受寒邪而得病，属于微邪；如果是因感受湿邪而得病，属于贼邪。

Why is this so? Suppose a person develops a heart disorder. If it arises from exposure to wind, it is classified as a deficiency pathogen. If it arises from summer-heat exposure, it is classified as a regular pathogen. If it arises from improper diet or overexertion, it is classified as an excess pathogen. If it arises from exposure to cold, it is classified as a slight pathogen. If it arises from dampness, it is classified as a thief pathogen.

💡 这是以心病为例，将五种病因与五邪分类对应。风对应肝木，暑对应心火，饮食劳倦对应脾土，寒对应肺金，湿对应肾水。这里的伤寒是古代广义感受寒邪的概念，不等同于现代医学的伤寒杆菌感染或现代疾病名称。中湿也不是单纯指现代医学中的某种湿度暴露疾病。此处体现的是古代病因学与五行脏腑配属的综合解释。现代医学不支持风、暑、湿等外邪直接按五行关系传入特定内脏的机制，但环境因素、营养状态、感染及疲劳确实可能影响人体生理功能。

舉心為例，以發明上文之義也。中風，肝木之邪也。得之，言因中風而心得病也。肝邪乘心，是從後來者，故曰虛邪。傷暑得之，為心臟自病，故曰正邪。飲食勞倦得之，脾邪乘心，是前來者，故曰實邪。傷寒得之，肺乘心，從所勝來者，故曰微邪。中濕得之，腎邪乘心，從所不勝來者，故曰賊邪。餘臟可類推，此病傳五臟之生克也。

这里举心脏为例，是为了阐明上文的道理。中风属于肝木之邪。所谓得之，是指因感受风邪而使心脏发生疾病。肝木之邪侵犯心脏，是从五行相生关系中的后方传来，因此称为虚邪。因伤暑而得病，是心脏自身感受暑邪而发病，因此称为正邪。因饮食不节、劳累过度而得病，是脾土之邪侵犯心脏，属于从前方传来，因此称为实邪。因伤寒而得病，是肺金之邪侵犯心脏，属于从心所能胜过的一方传来，因此称为微邪。因中湿而得病，是肾水之邪侵犯心脏，属于从心所不能胜过的一方传来，因此称为贼邪。其他脏腑也可以依此类推。这就是疾病按照五行生克关系在五脏之间传变的理论。

The heart is used as an example to clarify the principles discussed above. Wind-related illness is associated with Liver Wood. The expression de zhi means that the heart becomes diseased as a result of exposure to wind. When Liver Wood invades the heart, the pathogen comes from the generating side, which is why it is classified as a deficiency pathogen. Illness caused by summer-heat exposure is regarded as the heart's own disorder and is therefore classified as a regular pathogen. Illness caused by improper diet or overexertion involves Spleen Earth invading the heart. It comes from the receiving side and is therefore classified as an excess pathogen. Illness caused by cold involves Lung Metal invading the heart. It comes from the phase that the heart can overcome and is therefore classified as a slight pathogen. Illness caused by dampness involves Kidney Water invading the heart. It comes from the phase that the heart cannot overcome and is therefore classified as a thief pathogen. The other organs may be understood by analogy. This is the theory of disease transmission among the five zang organs according to generation and restriction.

💡 本段将抽象的五行关系落实到病因分类，是理解前文的关键。注意古代中风与现代脑卒中不是同一个概念：古代中风常泛指外风或风邪相关病证，不能直接翻译为现代 stroke。伤暑、伤寒、中湿同样应按古代病因学语境理解。考试可采用心病配属表记忆：风—肝—虚邪；暑—心—正邪；饮食劳倦—脾—实邪；寒—肺—微邪；湿—肾—贼邪。此处没有指定针灸穴位。现代临床辨证不能仅凭患者病因名称就判断其所属五邪，必须结合实际症状、舌脉及现代医学检查。

五十一難

第五十一难。

The Fifty-First Difficulty.

💡 本篇讨论根据患者对寒热环境、他人及声音的偏好，辅助辨别脏病与腑病。

五十一難曰︰病有欲得溫者，有欲得寒者，有欲得見人者，有不欲得見人者，而各不同，病在何臟腑也？

第五十一难问：有些患者希望得到温暖，有些患者希望得到寒凉；有些患者愿意见到别人，有些患者则不愿意见人。这些表现各不相同，那么疾病究竟发生在什么脏或腑呢？

The Fifty-First Difficulty asks: Some patients desire warmth, while others desire coolness. Some wish to see other people, while others do not wish to see anyone. Since these preferences differ, in which zang organ or fu organ does the illness occur?

💡 本段提出四种观察指标：喜温、喜寒、喜见人、不欲见人。它们属于古代望闻问切中通过行为、环境偏好和精神状态辅助判断脏腑病位的思路。这里的见人不仅是社交意愿，也涉及患者是否愿意接触外界刺激。现代临床可将寒热偏好、精神状态、活动意愿作为病史采集信息，但不能单独用来诊断脏腑疾病。

然︰病欲得寒，而欲得見人者，病在腑也；病欲得溫，而不欲見人者，病在臟也。何以言之？腑者陽也，陽病欲得寒，又欲見人；臟者陰也，陰病欲得溫，又欲閉戶獨處，惡聞人聲。故以別知臟腑之病也。

答：患者希望寒凉，并且愿意见人，疾病在腑；患者希望温暖，并且不愿意见人，疾病在脏。为什么这样说呢？腑属于阳，阳病容易出现热盛，因此希望寒凉，也愿意见人。脏属于阴，阴病容易出现寒盛，因此希望温暖，并且希望关门独处，厌恶听到别人的声音。所以可以根据这些表现来辨别脏病与腑病。

The answer is as follows: When a patient desires coolness and wishes to see other people, the disease is located in a fu organ. When a patient desires warmth and does not wish to see others, the disease is located in a zang organ. Why? The fu organs belong to Yang. In a Yang disorder, heat tends to predominate, so the patient desires coolness and also wishes to see people. The zang organs belong to Yin. In a Yin disorder, cold tends to predominate, so the patient desires warmth, wishes to remain alone behind closed doors, and dislikes hearing other people's voices. These tendencies can therefore be used to distinguish disorders of the zang and fu organs.

💡 核心是脏属阴、腑属阳的整体辨证模型。腑病喜寒、喜见人，解释为阳病热盛、阳主动而向外；脏病喜温、不欲见人，解释为阴病寒盛、阴主静而向内。这里的喜寒、喜温是患者主观偏好，不等同于客观体温变化。现代医学中，发热、畏寒、怕冷、畏光、声音敏感、社交退缩可能分别涉及感染、体温调节、神经系统、精神心理或全身状态，不能据此直接判定脏腑。考试重点是记忆两组对应关系。

《素問‧金匱真言論》曰︰腑者，陽也，臟者，陰也。腑為陽，陽病則熱勝，故飲食衣服居處，皆欲就寒而遠熱也。陽主動而散，以應乎外，故欲得見人也。臟為陰，陰病則寒勝，故飲食衣服居處，皆欲就溫而遠寒也，陰主靜而藏，以應乎內，故閉戶獨處，惡聞人聲也。此統論臟腑陰陽大義，故與陽明脈解篇陽明病惡人與火，指一經熱甚而煩惋者，有間也。

《素问·金匮真言论》说：腑属于阳，脏属于阴。腑属阳，阳病则热偏盛，所以在饮食、衣服和居住环境方面，都希望接近寒凉而远离炎热。阳主活动、发散，与外在环境相应，因此患者希望见人。脏属阴，阴病则寒偏盛，所以在饮食、衣服和居住环境方面，都希望接近温暖而远离寒冷。阴主安静、内藏，与内在环境相应，因此患者喜欢关门独处，厌恶听到别人的声音。这一段是从总体上讨论脏腑阴阳的大原则，因此它与《素问·阳明脉解篇》中所说阳明病患者厌恶见人和火热环境的情况有所不同。后者指的是某一经脉热邪极盛、患者烦闷不安的特殊表现。

The Suwen chapter Jingui Zhenyan Lun (True Words from the Golden Coffer) states that the fu organs belong to Yang and the zang organs belong to Yin. Since the fu organs are Yang, Yang disorders are characterized by predominating heat. Consequently, in matters of food, clothing, and living environment, the patient wishes to approach coolness and avoid heat. Yang governs movement and dispersion and corresponds to the external world; therefore, the patient wishes to see other people. Since the zang organs are Yin, Yin disorders are characterized by predominating cold. Consequently, in matters of food, clothing, and living environment, the patient wishes to approach warmth and avoid cold. Yin governs stillness and storage and corresponds to the interior; therefore, the patient wishes to remain alone behind closed doors and dislikes hearing other people's voices. This passage discusses the general principles of Yin and Yang in the zang-fu system. It therefore differs from the specific situation described in the Suwen chapter Yangming Mai Jie (Explanation of the Yangming Channel), where a patient with intense heat in the Yangming channel dislikes people and fire because of severe heat and distress.

💡 本段引用《黄帝内经·素问·金匮真言论》，以阴阳属性解释环境偏好。重要的是作者强调这里讨论的是脏腑阴阳的一般规律，而不是所有疾病都必须符合的固定表现。《阳明脉解篇》中的恶人、恶火属于阳明经热盛的特殊证候，不能与本段的脏腑总体分类混为一谈。现代医学中，热不耐受、畏寒、社会退缩和声音敏感均需结合病史及检查判断。考试常考一般规律与特殊经病的区别。

五十二難

第五十二难。

The Fifty-Second Difficulty.

💡 本难讨论脏病与腑病在病变位置、活动特点及症状表现上的区别。

五十二難曰︰腑臟發病，根本等不？

第五十二难问：脏与腑发生疾病时，它们的病变根本是否相同？

The Fifty-Second Difficulty asks: When disorders arise in the zang and fu organs, are their underlying characteristics the same?

💡 此处的根本主要指病变的性质、动态特点及病位表现，并非现代解剖学意义上的器官组织结构。

然︰不等也。其不等奈何？

答：并不相同。它们有哪些不同呢？

The answer is: They are not the same. In what way do they differ?

💡 这是问答体结构中的承接句，引出脏病与腑病的具体比较。

然︰臟病者，止而不移，其病不離其處；腑病者，仿佛賁響，上下行流，居處無常。故以此知臟腑根本不同也。

答：脏病的特点是病变相对固定，不容易移动，疾病不会离开原来的部位；腑病则好像有气体奔动、发出声响一样，症状上下流动，所在部位没有固定的位置。因此可以据此认识到脏病与腑病的根本性质不同。

The answer is: In zang disorders, the pathological condition remains relatively still and does not move; the illness stays in its original location. In fu disorders, there may be sensations resembling rushing movement or internal rumbling, with symptoms moving upward and downward and having no fixed location. This distinction shows that the underlying characteristics of zang and fu disorders are different.

💡 脏病止而不移，强调病位固定；腑病上下行流，强调气机运行、传导及症状位置变化。仿佛指类似、仿似；贲响指奔动并伴有声响，可联系腹中鸣响、气体运行等传统描述。现代医学中，固定性疼痛、游走性疼痛、肠鸣、腹胀和胃肠蠕动可以作为症状特征，但不能简单等同于脏病或腑病。临床上，疼痛位置固定或转移具有诊断价值，例如腹痛部位变化可能提示不同病理过程，但必须进行现代医学鉴别。

臟為陰，陰主靜，故止而不移也。腑為陽，陽主動，故上下流行也。仿佛，無形質也。賁響，動而有聲也。居無常處者，忽上忽下，即流行之謂也。臟病腑病，其根本不同者如此。

脏属于阴，阴主静，所以脏病表现为相对固定、不易移动。腑属于阳，阳主动，所以腑病表现为上下流动。仿佛，是指没有固定的实体形态；贲响，是指活动时伴有声音。所谓所在位置没有固定之处，就是指症状忽而向上、忽而向下，也就是流动变化的意思。脏病与腑病在根本性质上的区别就是如此。

The zang organs belong to Yin, and Yin governs stillness; therefore, their disorders tend to remain fixed and do not move. The fu organs belong to Yang, and Yang governs movement; therefore, their disorders tend to move upward and downward. The term fangfu describes something that has no definite physical form. Benxiang refers to movement accompanied by sound. To have no constant location means that symptoms may suddenly appear above or below, which is what is meant by movement and flow. This is how the fundamental differences between zang and fu disorders are understood.

💡 本段解释关键词：止而不移、上下流行、仿佛、贲响、居无常处。仿佛在这里不是现代汉语的好像，而强调症状无固定形质；贲响强调气机奔动及声响。理论基础仍是阴静阳动。现代胃肠道中的蠕动、肠鸣和气体移动可以帮助理解部分描述，但古代的腑病范围远大于现代胃肠动力学，不能直接一一对应。

五十三難

第五十三难。

The Fifty-Third Difficulty.

💡 本难讨论五脏疾病按照五行相克或相生关系传变时，对预后及生死判断的传统解释。

五十三難曰︰經言七傳者死，間臟者生，何謂也？

第五十三难问：医经说，疾病经过七传则会导致死亡，经过间脏传变则能够存活。这是什么意思？

The Fifty-Third Difficulty asks: The classical medical texts state that transmission through seven stages leads to death, whereas transmission through an intervening organ leads to survival. What does this mean?

💡 七传与间脏是古代五行病传理论中的预后概念。七传强调沿相克关系传变，间脏强调沿相生关系传变。此处的生死判断属于古代理论体系，不是现代医学中可直接应用的死亡预测规则。

然︰七傳者，傳其所勝也。間臟者，傳其子也。何以言之？假令心病傳肺、肺傳肝、肝傳脾、脾傳腎、腎傳心，一臟不再傷，故言七傳者死也。

答：所谓七传，就是疾病传向本脏所克制的脏。所谓间脏，就是疾病传向本脏所生养的子脏。为什么这样说呢？假设心病传到肺，肺病传到肝，肝病传到脾，脾病传到肾，肾病再传到心。如果同一个脏再次受到邪气损伤，就无法继续承受，因此说七传者会死亡。

The answer is: Seven-stage transmission means that the disease is transmitted to the organ that the affected organ restrains. Intervening-organ transmission means that the disease is transmitted to the organ that it generates. Why is this so? Suppose a heart disorder is transmitted to the lung, the lung disorder to the liver, the liver disorder to the spleen, the spleen disorder to the kidney, and the kidney disorder back to the heart. Since an organ cannot withstand being injured again, the text states that transmission through seven stages leads to death.

💡 七传按照相克次序运行：心火克肺金，肺金克肝木，肝木克脾土，脾土克肾水，肾水克心火，形成相克循环。原文以再次受邪、不能承受解释死亡。这里的七传不是简单地指七个不同脏器，因为五脏只有五个，而是指传变次序及再次受伤的阶段。其具体计算历来存在解释差异，下一段注释专门讨论七与次的文字问题。现代医学不认为器官按五行相克循环发生疾病传递，也没有以七传判断死亡的临床依据。考试应掌握相克次序及七传与间脏的区别。

七傳者，依序傳其所勝所克之臟也。如心病傳肺，是火克金也。肺又傳肝，是金克木也。肝又傳脾，是木克土也。脾又傳腎，是土克水也。腎複傳心，是水克火也。心又欲傳肺，是七傳矣。一臟不能再受邪傷，則死矣。呂廣以（七）當作（次）字之誤，與下間字方相合，其說亦通。蓋心病六傳，由腎至心，心臟不能複傳至肺也。其一臟不再傷者，是指心之不任再傷於第七傳而死也。此即《素問‧標本病傳論》諸病以次相傳者，皆有死期，不可刺之義。

七传，是指疾病按照顺序传向本脏所克制的脏。例如心病传肺，是火克金；肺病再传肝，是金克木；肝病再传脾，是木克土；脾病再传肾，是土克水；肾病再传心，是水克火；心又要传肺，这就构成了第七传。一个脏不能再次承受邪气损伤，否则就会死亡。吕广认为这里的七字应当是次字的讹误，这样就能与下文的间字相对应，这种解释也讲得通。因为心病经历六次传变，由肾再传回心，心脏便不能再次传到肺了。所谓一个脏不能再次受伤，是指心脏无法承受第七次传变时再次受到损伤，因此导致死亡。这与《素问·标本病传论》中所说的疾病按照次序相传，各有死亡期限，因而不可针刺的意思相通。

Seven-stage transmission means that the disease is transmitted in sequence to the organ that the current organ restrains. For example, a heart disorder is transmitted to the lung because Fire restrains Metal. The lung then transmits the disorder to the liver because Metal restrains Wood. The liver transmits it to the spleen because Wood restrains Earth. The spleen transmits it to the kidney because Earth restrains Water. The kidney transmits it back to the heart because Water restrains Fire. The heart would then transmit it to the lung again, completing what is described as the seventh transmission. An organ cannot withstand another injury, and death follows. Lü Guang considered the character qi, meaning seven, to be a textual error for ci, meaning sequence or stage. This interpretation corresponds more closely to the word jian, meaning intervening, in the following discussion and is also reasonable. According to this interpretation, a heart disorder undergoes six transmissions before returning from the kidney to the heart. The heart cannot then transmit the disease to the lung again. The statement that an organ cannot be injured a second time means that the heart cannot withstand another injury at the seventh stage, and death follows. This corresponds to the principle in the Suwen's Treatise on the Transmission of Root and Manifestation that diseases transmitted successively have their respective times of death and should not be treated with acupuncture.

💡 这是文本校勘与理论解释的重要段落。七传存在两种解释：一是按七次传变理解；二是吕广认为七是次的讹字，应理解为依次传变。作者进一步解释心病从心开始，沿相克链条传肺、肝、脾、肾，再回到心，心再次受伤则不能承受。引用《素问·标本病传论》，说明古代医家对严重传变及针刺禁忌的重视。注意原文不可刺之是特定古代理论语境下的警示，不能机械理解为现代临床凡重症皆不可针刺。现代针灸治疗须根据患者生命体征、病情稳定性、诊断及禁忌证决定；危重患者应优先接受急救和规范治疗。这里没有指定具体穴位。

間臟者，傳其所生也。假令心病傳脾、脾傳肺、肺傳腎、腎傳肝、肝傳心，是子母相傳，竟而複始，如環之無端，故曰生也。

所谓间脏，就是疾病传向本脏所生养的脏。假设心病传到脾，脾病传到肺，肺病传到肾，肾病传到肝，肝病再传到心。这是母子相传，循环一周后又重新开始，就像圆环一样没有终点，因此称为生。

Intervening-organ transmission means that the disease is transmitted to the organ generated by the affected organ. For example, a heart disorder is transmitted to the spleen, the spleen to the lung, the lung to the kidney, the kidney to the liver, and the liver back to the heart. This is transmission between parent and child organs. The cycle completes and begins again, like a ring without an endpoint. Hence, it is described as life or survival.

💡 间脏的传变顺序是相生循环：心火生脾土，脾土生肺金，肺金生肾水，肾水生肝木，肝木生心火。这里的子母关系是五行生化关系中的母子配属，并非解剖学上的器官生殖或实际组织生成。与前文相克传变相比，相生传变在古代理论中被认为较为有利，因而称生。现代医学没有证据表明疾病沿此顺序在器官之间传播。考试重点是熟记五行相生循环。

間臟者，間一臟傳其所生也。如心欲傳肺，而脾者肺之母，心之子，中間間此一臟，不傳所克也。假令心病傳脾，是間肺所勝之臟，為火生土也。脾病傳肺，是間腎所勝之臟，為土生金也。肺病傳腎，是間肝所勝之臟，為金生水也。腎病傳肝，是間心所勝之臟，為水生木也。肝病傳心，是間脾所勝之臟，為木生火也。心病又複傳脾，則病自已，此子母相傳而生也。

所谓间脏，就是隔开一个脏，再传向由本脏所生养的脏。例如心病本来可能传向肺，但脾是肺的母脏，同时又是心的子脏，因此在心与肺之间隔着脾，疾病不直接传向心所克制的肺，而是通过中间的脾传变。假设心病传脾，就是隔开肺这个心所克制的脏，因为这是火生土。脾病传肺，是隔开肾这个脾所克制的脏，因为这是土生金。肺病传肾，是隔开肝这个肺所克制的脏，因为这是金生水。肾病传肝，是隔开心这个肾所克制的脏，因为这是水生木。肝病传心，是隔开脾这个肝所克制的脏，因为这是木生火。心病再传脾，疾病便可以自行好转。这就是母子相传而有利于康复的道理。

Intervening-organ transmission means that the disease passes over one organ and is transmitted to the organ generated by the current organ. For example, a heart disorder might otherwise be transmitted to the lung. However, the spleen is the mother of the lung and the child of the heart. It therefore serves as the intervening organ, and the disease passes through it rather than directly following the restraining relationship. Suppose a heart disorder is transmitted to the spleen. The lung, which the heart restrains, has been bypassed, and the transmission follows Fire generating Earth. When a spleen disorder is transmitted to the lung, the kidney, which the spleen restrains, is bypassed, and Earth generates Metal. When a lung disorder is transmitted to the kidney, the liver, which the lung restrains, is bypassed, and Metal generates Water. When a kidney disorder is transmitted to the liver, the heart, which the kidney restrains, is bypassed, and Water generates Wood. When a liver disorder is transmitted to the heart, the spleen, which the liver restrains, is bypassed, and Wood generates Fire. If the heart disorder is then transmitted again to the spleen, the illness is said to resolve spontaneously. This is the beneficial transmission between parent and child organs.

💡 本段详细解释间脏的结构：疾病不是直接走相克路径，而是间隔一个脏，沿相生路径传变。五组对应关系必须掌握：心→脾，脾→肺，肺→肾，肾→肝，肝→心。注意原文心病传脾，是火生土；脾病传肺，是土生金；肺病传肾，是金生水；肾病传肝，是水生木；肝病传心，是木生火。此处的自已指疾病自行痊愈或病势趋于好转，是古代理论的预后判断，不代表所有相生关系的疾病都会自然康复。现代医学需根据病因、病程、病情严重程度评估预后。

五十四難

第五十四难。

The Fifty-Fourth Difficulty.

💡 本难讨论脏病难治、腑病易治的传统理论，以及疾病传变关系对治疗难易的影响。

五十四難曰︰臟病難冶，腑病易治，何謂也？

第五十四难问：为什么说脏病难以治疗，而腑病容易治疗呢？

The Fifty-Fourth Difficulty asks: Why is it said that disorders of the zang organs are difficult to treat, whereas disorders of the fu organs are easy to treat?

💡 原文冶为治的异文或传写问题，按语义应理解为治疗。此处提出脏病与腑病的治疗难易问题，重点并不在现代解剖器官的治疗难度，而在传统病位深浅及传变方向。

然︰臟病所以難治者，傳其所勝也；腑病易治者，傳其子也。與七傳間臟同法也。

答：脏病之所以难治，是因为它传向自身所克制的脏；腑病之所以容易治疗，是因为它传向自身所生养的子脏。这与七传和间脏的原理相同。

The answer is: Zang disorders are difficult to treat because they are transmitted to the organs that they restrain. Fu disorders are easier to treat because they are transmitted to their child organs. The principle is the same as that of seven-stage transmission and intervening-organ transmission.

💡 本段把第五十三难的传变理论用于解释治疗难易。脏病传所胜，属于相克传变，传统上认为病势较重；腑病传其子，属于相生传变，传统上认为病势较缓。需要注意，这是本书所建立的理论关联，不应将其当作现代临床的普遍规律。

臟病所以難治者，傳其所勝也，若傳其所生，亦易治也。腑病所以易治者，傳其所生也，若傳其所勝、亦難治也。蓋其義以臟病深，腑病淺，分其難易耳。然亦不可拘，故曰與七傳間臟同法也。

脏病之所以难治，是因为疾病传向其所克制的脏；如果脏病传向自身所生养的脏，也同样比较容易治疗。腑病之所以容易治疗，是因为疾病传向其所生养的脏；如果腑病传向自身所克制的脏，也会变得难治。总的来说，这一理论是以脏病病位较深、腑病病位较浅来区分治疗难易。不过也不能拘泥于此，所以说它与七传、间脏的原理相同。

Zang disorders are difficult to treat when they are transmitted to the organs that they restrain. If they are transmitted to the organs they generate, however, they are also relatively easy to treat. Fu disorders are considered easy to treat because they are transmitted to their child organs. If they are transmitted to the organs they restrain, they too become difficult to treat. The underlying rationale is that zang disorders are regarded as deeper and fu disorders as more superficial, and this distinction is used to explain their relative difficulty of treatment. Nevertheless, the principle should not be applied rigidly. That is why the text says that the same method of interpretation as seven-stage transmission and intervening-organ transmission applies.

💡 这一注释对前文作了重要补充：脏病不必然难治，腑病也不必然易治，关键还要看传变方向。脏病传所生，可较易治；腑病传所胜，则也可能难治。作者进一步指出脏病深、腑病浅是传统解释基础，但明确提醒不可拘泥。现代医学中，疾病难治程度取决于病因、组织损伤、疾病分期、并发症及治疗反应，不能以脏腑阴阳或五行传变单独决定。考试重点是理解其条件性，而不是死记脏难腑易。

五十五難

第五十五难。

The Fifty-Fifth Difficulty.

💡 本难区分积与聚，是传统腹部病证及气血病机的重要分类。

五十五難曰︰病有積有聚，何以別之？

第五十五难问：疾病有积和聚两种情况，应该怎样区别它们？

The Fifty-Fifth Difficulty asks: Some illnesses are classified as accumulation and others as gathering. How should they be distinguished?

💡 积与聚是古代病证术语，主要用于描述腹内病理性结聚及其不同表现。后世常将积聚与腹部肿块、胀痛等证候联系起来，但不可直接等同于现代医学的肿瘤。

然︰積者，陰氣也，聚者，陽氣也。故陰沉而伏，陽浮而動。氣之所積名曰積，氣之所聚名曰聚。故積者五臟所生，聚者六腑所成也。積者，陰氣也，其始發有常處，其痛不離其部，上下有所終始，左右有所窮處；聚者，陽氣也，其始發無根本，上下無所留止，其痛無常處，謂之聚。故以是別知積聚也。

答：积属于阴气，聚属于阳气。因此，阴性沉静而潜伏，阳性浮动而运行。气邪在体内逐渐积蓄形成的，称为积；气邪聚集形成的，称为聚。所以积是五脏所产生的病变，聚是六腑所形成的病变。积属于阴，其开始发病时有固定部位，疼痛不会离开所在区域，上下有明确的范围和界限，左右也有固定的边界。聚属于阳，其开始发病时没有固定的根本位置，上下没有固定停留之处，疼痛也没有恒定的位置，这就称为聚。因此，可以根据这些特点辨别积与聚。

The answer is: Accumulation, ji, belongs to Yin, whereas gathering, ju, belongs to Yang. Yin therefore tends to be deep and concealed, while Yang tends to be floating and moving. When pathogenic qi accumulates, it is called ji; when qi gathers, it is called ju. Thus, accumulation is associated with the five zang organs, while gathering is associated with the six fu organs. Accumulation belongs to Yin. From its onset, it has a relatively constant location, and its pain remains within that region. It has definite upper and lower boundaries and definite limits on the left and right. Gathering belongs to Yang. From its onset, it has no fixed origin, does not remain in one location as it moves upward and downward, and its pain has no constant location. This is called ju. These characteristics are used to distinguish accumulation from gathering.

💡 核心鉴别：积属阴、属五脏，位置固定，疼痛固定，边界相对明确；聚属阳、属六腑，位置游移，疼痛不定，变化较多。气之所积与气之所聚是古代气机病理概念。现代临床中，固定性腹部肿块、持续局限性疼痛与游走性腹痛、胃肠胀气等表现确实需要区别，但积不等于实体肿瘤，聚也不等于单纯功能性胃肠疾病。遇到持续腹部包块、进行性腹痛、消瘦、便血等情况，应及时进行影像学及必要的内镜检查。

積者，五臟所生，臟屬陰，陰邪漸積而成，故曰積。陰主靜，故沉伏不離其處，乃臟陰氣結為病，而或兼乎血，故其部上下左右，其形大小長短，皆可循而按之也。聚者，六腑所生，腑屬陽，陽邪漸聚而成，故曰聚。陽主動，故浮動而無定處，乃純乎氣凝滯而不散，故其部無定位，其體無定形。而上下左右，流行無常也，此陰陽積聚之所由分，與五十二難當是一章，或前後錯簡耳。

积是五脏所产生的病变。脏属于阴，阴邪逐渐积蓄形成病变，所以称为积。阴主静，因此积病沉伏而不离开原来的位置。这是脏的阴气凝结形成的病变，有时也可能兼有血分病变，所以其所在部位的上下左右范围，以及形状的大小、长短，都可以通过循摸和按压来检查。聚是六腑所产生的病变。腑属于阳，阳邪逐渐聚集形成病变，所以称为聚。阳主动，因此聚病浮动而没有固定位置。这主要是气凝滞而不能消散，所以其所在部位不固定，形态也不固定，上下左右流动变化，没有规律。这就是根据阴阳来区分积与聚的原因。此段与第五十二难应当原本属于同一章，或许是传抄时前后次序错乱造成的。

Accumulation arises from the five zang organs. Since the zang organs belong to Yin, Yin pathogens gradually accumulate and form the disorder called ji. Yin governs stillness, so accumulation remains deep and concealed without leaving its original location. It is a pathological condition caused by the binding of Yin qi within the zang organs and may also involve Blood. Therefore, its region, upper and lower boundaries, left and right limits, and its size and length can all be examined through palpation. Gathering arises from the six fu organs. Since the fu organs belong to Yang, Yang pathogens gradually gather and form the disorder called ju. Yang governs movement, so gathering moves and has no fixed location. It is described primarily as qi becoming congested and failing to disperse. Consequently, its location and physical form are not fixed, and it may move unpredictably upward, downward, leftward, or rightward. This is the basis for distinguishing accumulation and gathering through the concepts of Yin and Yang. This passage may originally have belonged to the same chapter as the Fifty-Second Difficulty, or the text may have become disordered during transmission.

💡 本段进一步解释积聚的病理与体征。积可能兼血，说明古代医家并非将所有积病都视为纯气病；聚则偏重气机凝滞。循而按之指沿着病变区域触诊，以确定形态、范围及边界。文末提出篇章错简的可能，属于古籍版本学与篇章结构问题。现代医学可将触诊、肿块边界、活动度及压痛作为检查内容，但腹部包块的性质必须通过超声、CT、MRI或其他适当检查明确。不能用阴阳分类代替病理诊断。

五十六難

第五十六难。

The Fifty-Sixth Difficulty.

💡 本难讨论五脏积病的名称、形态、相关症状及传统五行传变病因。

五十六難曰︰五臟之積，各有名乎？以何月何日得之？

第五十六难问：五脏所产生的积病，各自有名称吗？这些积病是在什么月份、什么日子形成的呢？

The Fifty-Sixth Difficulty asks: Does each of the five zang organs have its own named form of accumulation? In which months and on which days are these accumulations acquired?

💡 本难将五脏积病分别命名，并结合五行旺时、干支月份及日期解释其形成。这里的月份与日期属于古代五运六气及五行旺衰观念，不是现代流行病学所证实的发病时间规律。

然︰肝之積，名曰肥氣，在左脅下如覆杯，有頭足，久不愈，令人發咳逆瘧，連歲不已，以季夏戊己日得之。何以言之？

答：肝的积病称为肥气，位于左侧胁肋下方，形状像倒扣的杯子，有头部和足部。长期不愈，会使人出现咳嗽气逆和疟疾样发作，连续多年不能停止。古人认为这种病在季夏的戊日、己日形成。为什么这样说呢？

The answer is: The accumulation associated with the liver is called Feiqi, or Fat Qi. It is located below the left hypochondrium and resembles an inverted cup, with a head and feet. If it remains unresolved for a long time, it may cause coughing with upward counterflow and recurrent malaria-like attacks that continue for years. It is said to arise on the Wu or Ji days of late summer. Why is this so?

💡 肥气是五积之一，属肝积，位置在左胁下，形如覆杯，头足说明其具有古人认为可触及的实体形态。季夏指夏季末段，戊己属土，因此与脾土旺时相配。咳逆在传统理论中可联系肝经别脉贯膈上注于肺的关系；疟在这里指古代疟疾类周期性发作，不应直接等同于现代疟原虫感染。现代医学中，左上腹包块、咳嗽和周期性发热需要分别检查，不能将其统一归因于肝积。肥气并非现代肝脏肿瘤的同义词。

肺病傳於肝，肝當傳脾，脾季夏適王，王者不受邪，肝複欲還肺，肺不肯受，故留結為積，故知肥氣以季夏戊己日得之。

肺病传到肝之后，按照五行相克关系，肝本应再传到脾。但脾在季夏正当旺盛，旺盛的脏不容易接受邪气。于是肝的病邪又想返回肺，而肺也不愿接受它，因此邪气停留并凝结在肝，形成积病。所以认为肥气是在季夏的戊日、己日形成的。

When a lung disorder is transmitted to the liver, the liver should, according to the theory, transmit it onward to the spleen. However, the spleen is particularly vigorous in late summer, and an organ at the height of its seasonal strength does not readily receive pathogenic influence. The liver therefore attempts to send the pathogen back to the lung, but the lung does not accept it. The pathogen consequently remains and becomes bound in the liver, forming an accumulation. This is why Feiqi is said to arise on the Wu and Ji days of late summer.

💡 这里使用五行相克传变与季节旺衰理论解释邪气停留：肺金病传肝木，肝木再传脾土；但季夏土旺，脾不受邪，病邪试图返回肺，而肺也不接受，最终留结于肝。戊己为土干，季夏属土，因此构成时间解释。所谓旺者不受邪，是传统脏气旺衰理论，并非现代医学证明器官在某个月份具有免疫屏障增强。考试要理解肺→肝→脾的传变逻辑及季夏土旺的关系。

積，蓄也，言血氣不行，積蓄為病，亦由五邪相傳而成也。肥氣者，言其氣之肥盛也。左脅為肝木左升之部，如覆杯者，本大末小，肝木之象也。頭足者，一本二末，木形歧出之義，亦甚言其有形也。咳逆者，足厥陰之別脈，貫膈上注於肺，肝氣上沖於肺，反乘所勝也。瘧即瘧，間二日發者是也。五臟皆有瘧，在肝則為風瘧，又瘧多發於少陽，而厥陰於少陽為表裡也。病邪入深，連年不已。然何以得之？乃肺病傳肝，傳其所勝也。肝當傳脾，脾土適旺於季夏，土旺力能拒而不受邪，當複反於肺，而肝木又不能勝肺金，故曰不肯受也。邪因無道可行，故留結於肝而成積矣。季夏戊己日得之者，季夏，未土月也。戊己，土日也，月日皆脾土極旺之時，肝木不能克製，即於是月是日，而得是積也。可見虛則受邪，旺則邪不得入，今人徒事攻積，大失經旨，非其治矣。此章唯出五積之名狀，而不言諸聚者，蓋聚無常處，故無名狀可定也。

积，就是蓄积的意思，指血和气运行不畅，逐渐积蓄而形成疾病，也可能由五种邪气在脏腑之间传变而形成。肥气，是指肝气积聚而显得肥盛。左侧胁肋是肝木之气向左上升的部位。形状像倒扣的杯子，是因为上部较大、末端较小，象征肝木的形态。所谓有头有足，是指一个根部、两个分出的末端，体现树木分枝的形态，也强调病变具有可触及的实体。咳嗽气逆，是因为足厥阴肝经的别脉贯穿膈肌并向上注入肺部，肝气上冲于肺，反过来侵犯其所克制的脏。所谓疟，就是间隔两日发作一次的疟疾样疾病。五脏都可能出现疟疾样病证，在肝则称为风疟；而疟疾多发于少阳经相关病证，厥阴与少阳互为表里。邪气深入后，病程可持续多年而不愈。那么这种积病为什么会形成呢？因为肺病传肝，是传给它所克制的脏（金克木）。肝本应传脾，但季夏脾土正旺，土旺之力能够抵抗邪气而不接受它。邪气于是想返回肺，而肝木又不能胜过肺金，所以说肺不接受。邪气没有其他道路可传，便停留凝结在肝而形成积病。所谓在季夏戊己日形成，是因为季夏属于未土之月，戊己属于土日，月份和日期都是脾土极旺的时期，肝木不能克制旺土，因此在这个月、这个日子形成这种积病。由此可以看出，虚弱时容易受到邪气侵袭，旺盛时邪气不容易进入。如今有些人只知道一味攻逐积病，严重违背经典的原意，并不是正确的治疗方法。本章只列举五积的名称和形态，没有列举各种聚病，因为聚没有固定位置，因此无法规定其固定名称和形态。

Ji means accumulation or storage. It describes a condition in which the movement of Qi and Blood becomes impaired, causing them to accumulate and produce illness. Such accumulation may also result from the transmission of the five pathogenic influences between organs. Feiqi refers to the apparently abundant or enlarged accumulation of qi. The left hypochondrium is regarded as the region where Liver Wood ascends on the left. Its inverted-cup shape, broad at the top and narrow at the bottom, symbolizes the form of Wood. The description of a head and feet refers to one root dividing into two branches, resembling the branching structure of a tree; it also emphasizes that the accumulation is tangible. Coughing with counterflow is explained through the collateral vessel of the Foot Jueyin Liver channel, which passes through the diaphragm and ascends to connect with the lung. Liver qi surges upward into the lung and attacks the organ that it restrains. The term nue refers to a malaria-like disorder whose attacks recur after a two-day interval. All five zang organs may be associated with malaria-like disorders. When associated with the liver, it is called wind malaria. Such disorders are also frequently associated with the Shaoyang system, which is exterior-interiorly related to Jueyin. When the pathogen penetrates deeply, the illness may persist for years. Why does this accumulation arise? The lung disorder is transmitted to the liver through a restraining relationship. The liver should then transmit the disorder to the spleen. However, the spleen is particularly vigorous during late summer, and vigorous Earth can resist and refuse the pathogen. The pathogen then attempts to return to the lung, but Liver Wood cannot overcome Lung Metal, so the lung does not accept it. With no further route of transmission, the pathogen remains bound in the liver and forms an accumulation. Feiqi is said to arise on Wu and Ji days in late summer because late summer corresponds to the Earth month of Wei, while Wu and Ji are Earth days. Both the month and the day represent a time when Spleen Earth is particularly vigorous. Liver Wood cannot overcome this vigorous Earth, and the accumulation is therefore said to form at that time. This illustrates the principle that deficiency allows pathogens to enter, whereas vigor prevents their entry. The author criticizes practitioners who simply attack and eliminate accumulations without considering the classical principles, arguing that this is not appropriate treatment. This chapter lists the names and forms of the five accumulations but does not describe individual forms of gathering, because gathering has no fixed location and therefore cannot be assigned a definite form or name.

💡 这是肥气的核心注释，内容涉及病机、形态、经脉循行、疟疾、季节旺衰及治疗原则。1. 积：气血运行不畅而蓄积。2. 肥气：肝积名称，形如覆杯，有头足，象征木的分枝形态。3. 足厥阴肝经别脉：传统经脉理论中与膈、肺相关，借此解释肝病影响肺而出现咳逆。4. 风疟：古代疟疾分类，不等同于现代疟原虫感染的特定类型。原文间二日发是古代对周期性发作的描述。5. 五行季节：季夏未月属土，戊己属土，脾土旺而不受邪。6. 治疗警示：作者反对不辨虚实、只知攻积。现代医学中，腹部肿块必须先明确病因，不能因古代积病理论而盲目攻下、活血或针刺。现代肝脏肿大、肝囊肿、肿瘤、脾脏病变及腹腔肿块均需鉴别。足厥阴肝经WHO代码为LR；原文没有具体穴位，不能随意补造穴位处方。考试重点：肥气属肝积，左胁下，形如覆杯，季夏戊己日，咳逆、疟，五行传变解释为邪气留结。

心之積，名曰伏梁，起臍上，大如臂，上至心下，久不愈，令人病煩心，以秋庚辛日得之。

心的积病称为伏梁，起始于肚脐上方，大小约如手臂，从脐上向上延伸至心脏下方。长期不愈，会使人出现心中烦闷的症状。古人认为这种积病是在秋季的庚日、辛日形成的。

The accumulation associated with the heart is called Fuliang, or Hidden Beam. It begins above the navel, is approximately the size of an arm, and extends upward to the region below the heart. If it persists for a long time, it causes distress or agitation in the heart. It is said to arise on Geng and Xin days in autumn.

💡 伏梁是五积之一，属心积。位置从脐上延伸至心下，形状如梁木横亘，大小如臂。烦心指心中烦闷、躁扰，不宜直接解释为现代心脏病。秋属金，庚辛属金，金旺克制心火，因此按照五行理论解释其形成。伏梁不是现代医学某一种明确疾病的专名。现代出现腹部肿块、心悸、胸闷或烦躁，应分别进行腹部和心血管系统评估。

何以言之？腎病傳心，心當傳肺，肺以秋適王，王者不受邪，心複欲還腎，腎不肯受，故留結為積。故知伏梁以秋庚辛日得之。

为什么这样说呢？肾病传到心之后，按照五行相克关系，心本应再传到肺。但肺在秋季正当旺盛，旺盛的脏不容易接受邪气。于是心的病邪又想返回肾，而肾也不愿接受，所以邪气停留凝结，形成积病。因此认为伏梁是在秋季的庚日、辛日形成的。

Why is this so? A kidney disorder is transmitted to the heart. The heart should then transmit it to the lung. However, the lung is particularly vigorous in autumn, and an organ at the height of its seasonal strength does not readily accept pathogenic influence. The heart therefore attempts to return the pathogen to the kidney, but the kidney does not accept it. The pathogen consequently remains and becomes bound, forming an accumulation. This is why Fuliang is said to arise on Geng and Xin days in autumn.

💡 传变链条为肾水→心火→肺金。心火本应克肺金，但秋季肺金旺盛，不受邪；邪气返回肾水，而肾也不接受，于是留结于心。秋属金，庚辛属金，体现季节五行旺衰理论。现代医学没有证据表明秋季肺功能旺盛会阻止病邪进入，也没有证据表明腹部积块按此循环形成。考试重点是理解伏梁的传变逻辑及秋金旺。

伏梁者，伏而不動，橫亙如梁木然，起臍上至心下者，臍上至心下，皆心之分部也。煩心者，火鬱則心煩也。然何以得之？乃腎病傳心，傳其所勝也。心當傳肺，肺金當秋適旺，金旺力能拒而不受邪，應複反於腎，而心火又不能勝腎水，故曰不肯受也。邪留結於心而成積，以秋庚辛日得之者，秋當申酉金月，而庚辛金日也。金旺之月日，心火不能克製，即於是月是日而得是積也。

伏梁，是指邪气潜伏而不移动，横向延伸，形状像横放的梁木。从脐上到心下这一范围，都属于心脏在传统脏腑分部中的区域。所谓烦心，是因为火气郁结而导致心中烦闷。那么这种病为什么会形成呢？这是因为肾病传到心，是传给它所克制的脏（水克火）。心本应继续传到肺，但肺金在秋季正当旺盛，金气旺盛，能够抵抗而不接受邪气。邪气理应返回肾，而心火又不能胜过肾水，所以说肾不肯接受。邪气于是留滞凝结在心而形成积病。所谓在秋季庚辛日形成，是因为秋季对应申月、酉月这两个金旺的月份，庚辛又是金日。金旺的月份和日期，心火不能克制，因此就在这样的月日形成伏梁。

Fuliang means that the pathological influence lies concealed and remains immobile, extending horizontally like a beam of wood. The region from above the navel to below the heart is regarded as belonging to the heart's functional territory in the traditional zang-fu system. Heart distress is explained as the result of Fire becoming constrained and producing agitation. How does this accumulation arise? A kidney disorder is transmitted to the heart through a restraining relationship. The heart should then transmit it to the lung. However, Lung Metal is particularly vigorous in autumn and is strong enough to resist and refuse the pathogen. The pathogen should therefore return to the kidney, but Heart Fire cannot overcome Kidney Water, so the kidney does not accept it. The pathogen remains bound in the heart and forms an accumulation. The statement that Fuliang arises on Geng and Xin days in autumn is based on the correspondence of autumn with the Metal months of Shen and You, while Geng and Xin are Metal days. During these months and days, Metal is vigorous and Heart Fire cannot overcome it. Thus, the accumulation is said to form at that time.

💡 本段解释伏梁的形态、病位和五行形成机制。伏而不动、横亘如梁是形态描述；脐上至心下属于传统心脏分部，不等于现代心脏解剖位置。火郁则心烦是五行病机解释。申、酉属秋金，庚、辛属金，金旺则心火受制。注意原文说肾不肯受邪，是传变模型中的关系，不应误读为肾脏具有主动拒绝病原体的现代生理机制。现代若发现腹部包块，尤其伴随心烦、胸部症状，应分别检查腹腔、心血管及其他系统。

按︰《靈樞‧邪氣臟腑病形篇》曰︰心脈微緩為伏梁，在心下上下行，時唾血。經筋篇曰︰手少陰之筋，其病內急，心承伏梁，下為肘網，其成伏梁吐膿血者，死不治。是《靈樞》兩章，皆心病有餘之積，雖未明言病狀，其義則同。若《素問‧腹中論》曰︰病有少腹上下左右皆有根，病名伏梁，裡大膿血，居腸胃之外，不可治，治之每切按之致死。此下則因陰，必下膿血，上則迫胃脘，生膈挾胃脘內癰，此久病也，難治。居臍上為逆，居臍下為從。此病陽邪聚於血分，致氣失輸轉之機，非臟陰氣結之積也。以其在少腹四旁太沖部分，陽毒之邪，聚而為膿為血，下行必薄陰中，便下膿血，上行迫胃脘膈膜間而生內癰，此論陽毒之伏梁也。又曰︰人有身體髀股皆腫，環臍而痛，病名伏梁，此風根也，其氣溢於大腸，而著於肓，肓之原在臍下，故環臍而痛也，不可動，動之為水溺澀之病。此病風邪根聚於中，故環臍而痛，臍為人身之樞，樞病則不能斡旋陰陽之氣，故周身皆腫。設妄攻風氣，鼓動其水，水溢於上，則小便為之不利，此論風毒之伏梁也。是其名雖同，其証其治則異，若伏梁不辨乎風根，其不見誚於雞峰難矣。

按语：《灵枢·邪气脏腑病形篇》说：心脉略见缓象，是伏梁，病变位于心下，可以上下移动，有时会吐血。《灵枢·经筋篇》说：手少阴心经之筋发生病变时，会出现内侧拘急，心部承受伏梁之病，向下影响肘部筋脉，形成肘部筋脉拘急；如果伏梁发展到吐出脓血的程度，就会死亡，无法治疗。这说明《灵枢》的这两篇都在讨论心病邪气有余所形成的积病，虽然没有完全明确描述病状，但基本意义相同。另一方面，《素问·腹中论》说：有一种疾病，少腹上下左右都有固定根部，名叫伏梁，内部有大量脓血，位于肠胃之外，不能治疗；如果治疗时反复用力切按，就可能导致死亡。病变向下发展，就会损伤阴部，必然排出脓血；向上发展，则会压迫胃脘，在膈与胃脘之间形成内痈。这是久病，难以治疗。病变位于脐上属于逆证，位于脐下属于顺证。这种疾病是阳邪聚集在血分，导致气的输送和转运功能失常，并不是脏阴之气凝结形成的积病。由于病变位于少腹四周、冲脉（太冲）所过的区域，阳毒之邪聚集后形成脓和血。向下发展，就会逼近阴部，出现排脓血；向上发展，则压迫胃脘和膈膜之间，形成内痈。这是在讨论阳毒所形成的伏梁。另一种说法是：有人全身、髀部和大腿都肿胀，绕着肚脐疼痛，这种病也叫伏梁，是风邪的根本潜伏之处。风邪之气溢入大肠，附着在肓部。肓的原穴在脐下，因此出现绕脐疼痛。这种病不能轻易发动治疗，若妄动病邪，就会出现小便涩滞不畅。此病是风邪根聚于身体内部，所以出现绕脐疼痛。肚脐是人体气机运行的枢纽，枢纽发生病变，就不能协调阴阳之气，因此全身都会肿胀。如果错误地使用攻逐风邪的方法，鼓动体内水液，水邪向上泛溢，就会造成小便不利。这是在讨论风毒形成的伏梁。由此可知，虽然这些病证名称相同，但证候和治疗方法却不一样。如果辨证伏梁时不区分风邪根聚的情况，就难免受到《鸡峰》一书作者的讥评。

A commentary follows. The Treatise on Pathogenic Influences and the Forms of Zang-Fu Disorders in the Lingshu states that a slightly moderate heart pulse indicates Fuliang, which lies below the heart and may extend upward and downward; the patient may occasionally spit blood. The Treatise on Sinew Channels states that disease of the sinew of the Hand Shaoyin channel may cause internal tightness. The heart bears the condition known as Fuliang, and downward involvement may produce a network-like tension around the elbow. If Fuliang develops to the point of producing pus and blood through vomiting, the condition is fatal and cannot be treated. These two passages in the Lingshu both describe an accumulation associated with excess in the heart. Although their descriptions of the symptoms are not identical, their underlying meanings are similar. The Suwen's Treatise on Abdominal Disorders, however, describes another condition called Fuliang. In this disorder, there is a fixed root extending in all directions through the lower abdomen. It contains abundant pus and blood and is located outside the intestines and stomach. It is considered untreatable, and repeated forceful palpation during treatment may cause death. If the disease extends downward, it affects the genital region and results in the discharge of pus and blood. If it extends upward, it compresses the stomach region and produces an internal abscess between the diaphragm and the stomach. This is a chronic and difficult-to-treat condition. A location above the navel is considered an unfavorable pattern, whereas a location below the navel is considered more favorable. This disorder results from Yang pathogenic influence gathering in the Blood aspect, impairing the mechanism by which Qi is transported and transformed. It is not an accumulation caused by the binding of Yin qi within the zang organs. Because the condition is located around the lower abdomen, in the region traversed by the Chong vessel (Taichong), pathogenic Yang toxin gathers and forms pus and blood. When it descends, it approaches the genital region and causes the discharge of pus and blood. When it ascends, it presses upon the stomach and diaphragm and produces an internal abscess. This is the Fuliang caused by Yang toxin. Another passage states that some people develop swelling throughout the body, including the hips and thighs, together with pain around the navel. This condition is also called Fuliang and is described as having a root of Wind. Its qi overflows into the large intestine and attaches to the Huang region. The source of Huang is located below the navel, which explains the circumferential pain around the navel. The condition must not be disturbed; if it is forcibly mobilized, difficulty and obstruction of urination may result. In this disorder, Wind pathogens have accumulated at the body's interior, producing pain around the navel. The navel is regarded as the body's central pivot. If this pivot is diseased, it cannot regulate and coordinate Yin and Yang qi, and generalized swelling may occur. If Wind qi is recklessly attacked and driven, the movement may disturb the body's fluids. If these fluids overflow upward, urination becomes difficult. This describes Fuliang caused by Wind toxin. Although the same name is used, the patterns and treatments are different. If a practitioner fails to distinguish the Fuliang associated with a root of Wind, the practitioner may deserve the criticism expressed in the text known as Jifeng.

💡 这是全段最重要的文献辨析之一：伏梁虽同名，却至少涉及不同病机，不能混为一谈。第一类： 《灵枢·邪气脏腑病形篇》所述心脉微缓、心下上下行、时唾血；《灵枢·经筋篇》所述手少阴心经筋病、吐脓血，均被注家归为心病有余的积。第二类：《素问·腹中论》所述少腹固定根部、脓血、肠胃之外的内痈，注家解释为阳毒聚于血分，非脏阴气结。第三类：全身髀股肿胀、环脐痛、小便涩滞，注家解释为风毒根聚于中。三个类型虽共用伏梁之名，但证候、病位及治疗原则不同。特别注意：1. 此处“太冲”指冲脉所过的少腹部位，不是足背上的太冲穴（LR3），不可据此取太冲治伏梁。2. 肓之原在脐下，历代多以脐下气海CV6当之，但原文并未在此处指定这些穴位，不能将其作为原文处方。3. 手少阴心经WHO代码HT；足厥阴肝经WHO代码LR；任脉WHO代码CV。原文未明确指定某一针刺穴位。4. 现代医学中，腹腔脓肿、腹膜后肿瘤、肠系膜肿块、腹部感染、肝脾肿大及腹水等都可能出现腹部包块、疼痛或腹胀，必须进行影像学及必要的病理学诊断。5. 原文所谓不可治、动之致死等反映古代对严重腹腔疾病的认识，不应直接作为今天的治疗禁忌。现代腹部包块若伴发热、持续腹痛、呕吐、便血、脓血便或排尿障碍，应及时就医。6. 鸡峰指《鸡峰普济方》等相关医籍传统，注家以此强调辨别同名异病的重要性。考试重点：伏梁至少要辨心积、阳毒伏梁、风毒伏梁，名称相同不代表病机相同。

脾之積，名曰痞氣，在胃脘，覆大如盤，久不愈，令人四肢不收，發黃癉，飲食不為肌膚，以冬壬癸日得之。何以言之？肝病傳脾，脾當傳腎，腎以冬適王，王者不受邪，脾複欲還肝，肝不肯受，故留結為積。故知痞氣以冬壬癸日得之。

脾脏的积聚病，称为痞气，病位在胃脘部，积块覆盖的范围大如盘子。长久不愈，会使人四肢不能正常活动，出现黄疸，饮食摄入的营养不能转化为肌肉。此病在冬季壬日或癸日发生。为什么这样说呢？肝病传给脾，按照五行相传的规律，脾本应再传给肾。肾在冬季正当旺盛，当令旺盛的脏气能够抵御邪气，不接受病邪。脾的邪气又想返回肝，但肝不肯接受，于是邪气停留凝结，形成积聚。因此知道，痞气与冬季壬日、癸日相联系。

The accumulation of the Spleen is called Pi Qi (spleen accumulation). It is located in the region of the Stomach, and its extent is as large as a plate. If it persists without recovery, it may cause impaired movement of the four limbs, jaundice, and failure of food to nourish the flesh. It is said to arise on Ren or Gui days in winter. Why is this so? A Liver disorder transmits to the Spleen. The Spleen should then transmit the disorder to the Kidney. In winter, the Kidney is at its seasonal peak, and an organ that is flourishing in its season does not receive pathogenic influences. The pathogenic influence of the Spleen then attempts to return to the Liver, but the Liver does not accept it. Consequently, it remains and congeals into an accumulation. Thus Pi Qi is associated with Ren and Gui days in winter.

💡 痞气是五积之一，属古代脏腑积聚理论。痞，含闭塞、痞结之意；积，指病理性积聚。胃脘是上腹部、胃所在的区域，不宜直接等同于现代解剖学上的某一个器官病变。覆大如盘是古代对积块范围的描述，不是现代影像学尺寸。四肢不收可理解为四肢活动不利或功能失常；黄瘅即黄疸；饮食不为肌肤，指脾主运化、肌肉失养的传统病机。冬属水，壬癸亦属水，故与肾相应。此处采用五行母子、胜复及季节旺衰解释疾病传变，是古代五运五行医学模型，不能当作现代疾病发生时间的统计规律。现代医学中，黄疸可能涉及肝胆疾病、溶血或其他原因；四肢无力、消瘦及腹部肿块也需要分别鉴别诊断。没有提供可直接对应的特定针灸穴位，不能仅凭痞气名称制定治疗方案。

痞者，否也，天地不交而為否，言痞結而成積也。脾位中央，土之象也，故積在胃脘，覆大如盤。睥主四肢，邪氣壅聚，正氣不運，故四肢不收。脾有濕滯，則色征於外，故皮膚爪目皆黃而成癉，但黃癉之因甚繁，然皆不離乎脾與濕也。脾主肌肉，今脾有積，不能布津液，則所入飲食，而不為肌膚也。然何以得之？乃肝病傳脾，傳其所勝也。脾當傳腎，腎水當冬適旺，水旺力能拒不受邪，欲複反於肝，而脾土又不能勝肝木，故曰不肯受也。邪留結於脾而成積。

痞，就是闭塞不通的意思，如同天地之气不能交合而形成《否》卦所象征的闭塞状态，这里是指气机痞塞凝结而形成积聚。脾居身体中央，五行属土，所以积聚位于胃脘，范围大如盘子。脾主四肢，邪气壅塞聚集，正常气机不能运行，因此四肢活动不利。脾有湿邪停滞，病色便表现在体表，所以皮肤、指甲和眼睛都可能发黄，形成黄疸。不过黄疸的原因很多，传统理论认为其病机多与脾和湿有关。脾主肌肉，现在脾有积聚，不能输布津液，于是吃进去的食物不能充分转化为肌肉。这种病为什么发生？是肝病传给脾，按照五行相胜的方向传变。脾本应传给肾，但冬季肾水正旺，水旺能抵御病邪，不接受邪气。病邪想再返回肝，而脾土又不能胜过肝木，所以说肝不肯接受。邪气于是停留凝结在脾而形成积聚。

Pi means obstruction or blockage. It is compared with the Pi hexagram, in which Heaven and Earth fail to communicate. Here it describes obstruction and congealing that develop into an accumulation. The Spleen occupies the center and corresponds to Earth in the Five Phases; therefore, the accumulation is located in the epigastric region and is as large as a plate. The Spleen governs the four limbs. When pathogenic influences obstruct and gather, the normal movement of Qi is impaired, and the limbs become difficult to move. When Dampness stagnates in the Spleen, its pathological color manifests externally, so the skin, nails, and eyes may appear yellow, producing jaundice. However, jaundice has many causes, although this traditional explanation associates it with the Spleen and Dampness. The Spleen governs the flesh. When an accumulation impairs its function of distributing fluids, food cannot adequately nourish the flesh. Why does this occur? A Liver disorder transmits to the Spleen according to the Five-Phase relationship of conquest. The Spleen should transmit the disorder to the Kidney, but Kidney Water is flourishing in winter and can resist the pathogenic influence. The influence attempts to return to the Liver, but Spleen Earth cannot overcome Liver Wood. Therefore, the Liver does not accept it, and the pathogenic influence remains and congeals in the Spleen to form an accumulation.

💡 本段以《易经》否卦解释痞塞，将易学、五行学说与脏腑病机结合。原文“睥”为“脾”字之误。脾主四肢、脾主肌肉，是中医藏象学说的重要内容。津液指人体正常水液；布津液是输布水液。五行相胜：木克土、土克水、水克火、火克金、金克木。这里的肝木克脾土，并非现代解剖学上肝脏直接破坏脾脏。现代医学不认为黄疸必然由脾湿造成，也不认为五行季节旺衰能预测疾病传播。黄疸应检查胆红素及肝胆、血液等相关指标。

以冬壬癸日得之者，冬當亥子水月，而壬癸水日也，水旺之月日，脾土不能克製，即於是月是日，而得是積也。

所谓在冬季壬日、癸日发病，是因为冬季对应亥月、子月，壬日和癸日又属水。在水气旺盛的月份和日子，脾土无法制约水，因此就在这些月份和日子形成这种积聚。

The statement that the condition arises on Ren or Gui days in winter refers to the correspondence of winter with the Hai and Zi months, which belong to Water, and of Ren and Gui days with the Water phase. During months and days when Water is flourishing, Spleen Earth is unable to restrain it, and the accumulation is said to develop at that time.

💡 此处是五行与干支历法的配属：亥、子为水月，壬、癸为水日，脾属土。冬季水旺、土受制，是传统病因推演。需要区分季节与干支理论的象征性解释，以及真正可观察的疾病风险因素。现代医学没有证据支持冬季壬癸日会增加脾脏或胃部积聚的发生率。

肺之積，名曰息賁，在右脅下，覆大如杯。久不愈，令人洒淅寒熱，喘咳，發肺壅，以春甲乙日得之。何以言之？心病傳肺，肺當傳肝，肝以春適王，王者不受邪，肺複欲還心，心不肯受，故留結為積。故知息賁以春甲乙日得之。

肺脏的积聚称为息贲，位于右胁下方，积块覆盖的范围如杯子大小。长久不愈，会使人出现阵阵恶寒发热、喘息、咳嗽，甚至发展为肺痈。此病在春季甲日或乙日发生。为什么这样说呢？心病传给肺，肺本应再传给肝。肝在春季正当旺盛，旺盛的脏气不接受病邪。肺的邪气又想返回心，但心不肯接受，于是邪气停留凝结形成积聚。因此知道息贲与春季甲日、乙日相联系。

The accumulation of the Lung is called Xi Ben (lung accumulation). It is located below the right hypochondrium and occupies an area about the size of a cup. If it persists, it may cause chills and fever, wheezing, coughing, and the development of a pulmonary abscess. It is said to arise on Jia or Yi days in spring. Why is this so? A Heart disorder transmits to the Lung, and the Lung should then transmit it to the Liver. The Liver is flourishing in spring and therefore does not receive the pathogenic influence. The influence in the Lung attempts to return to the Heart, but the Heart does not accept it. Consequently, it remains and congeals into an accumulation. Thus Xi Ben is associated with Jia and Yi days in spring.

💡 息贲是五积之一，贲在古代有奔迫、急迫之义，息贲强调呼吸气息奔迫。右胁下是古代对肺积位置的描述，不能简单等同于右肺某一叶。洒淅寒热指恶寒与发热交替或阵发；肺壅在下文解作肺痈。肺痈是中医病名，常见表现包括咳嗽、胸痛、发热、咳吐脓血。现代医学肺脓肿是可能的对照之一，但二者诊断标准并不完全相同。呼吸困难、持续咳嗽、发热或咳血应接受现代医学评估。春属木，甲乙属木，肺属金，肝属木，这一段仍以五行季节旺衰解释病邪传变，并非现代病理机制。

賁，古通奔。息賁者，言氣息賁迫也。右脅下為肺金右降之分部，洒淅寒熱者，肺主皮毛也。

贲在古代可以通作奔。息贲，就是形容呼吸之气急促奔迫。右胁下是传统理论中肺金之气向右下降的部位。阵阵恶寒发热，是因为中医认为肺主皮毛，肺的病变可以影响体表的卫外功能。

In ancient usage, the character ben in this name could be used interchangeably with another ben, meaning to rush or surge. Xi Ben therefore describes breath or Qi rushing urgently. The area below the right hypochondrium is regarded as the region associated with the rightward descending movement of Lung Metal. Alternating chills and fever are explained by the traditional principle that the Lung governs the skin and body hair.

💡 贲与奔的通假关系是理解病名的关键。肺主皮毛属中医藏象理论，指肺与卫气、汗液及体表防御功能的整体关系。现代医学中的呼吸系统与皮肤免疫功能存在复杂联系，但不能直接等同于肺脏控制皮肤。肺金右降是传统气机升降理论，不是解剖学上的肺部运动方向。

壅，癰，古通。肺病則喘咳，甚則發為肺癰。《素問‧大奇論》曰︰肺之壅，喘而兩滿者是也。然何以得之？乃心病傳肺，傳其所勝也。肺當傳肝，肝木當春適旺，木旺力能拒而不受邪，複欲反於心，肺金又不能勝心火，故曰不肯受也。邪留結於肺而成積，以春甲乙日得之者，春當寅卯木月，而甲乙木日也，木旺之月日，肺金不能克製，即於是月是日而得是積也。

壅在古代可以通作痈。肺病会出现喘息和咳嗽，严重时可能形成肺痈。《素问·大奇论》说：肺部壅塞，表现为喘息，而且两侧胸胁胀满，就是这种情况。那么息贲为什么发生？是心病传给肺，按照五行相胜的方向传变。肺本应传给肝，但肝木在春季正当旺盛，木旺能够抵御邪气，不接受病邪。邪气又想返回心，而肺金不能胜过心火，因此心也不接受。邪气于是停留凝结在肺而形成积聚。所谓在春季甲日、乙日发生，是因为春季对应寅月、卯月，甲乙日属木。在木气旺盛的月份和日子，肺金不能制约木，因此就在这些月份和日子形成这种积聚。

In ancient usage, yong, "obstruction," was interchangeable with the homophone yong, "abscess". Lung disease causes wheezing and coughing; in severe cases, it may develop into a pulmonary abscess. The Suwen, in the chapter 'Daqi Lun', states that Lung obstruction manifests as wheezing and fullness on both sides of the chest. Why does this condition occur? A Heart disorder transmits to the Lung according to the Five-Phase relationship of conquest. The Lung should transmit it to the Liver. Liver Wood flourishes in spring and can resist the pathogenic influence. The influence then attempts to return to the Heart, but Lung Metal cannot overcome Heart Fire. Thus the Heart does not accept it. The pathogenic influence remains and congeals in the Lung to form an accumulation. Its association with Jia and Yi days in spring is explained by the correspondence of spring with the Yin and Mao months and of Jia and Yi days with Wood. When Wood is flourishing, Lung Metal is unable to restrain it, and the accumulation is said to develop during those months and days.

💡 《素问·大奇论》是《黄帝内经》篇章，原文引用用来支持肺壅、喘、胸胁满的症候描述。肺痈在传统医学中属肺部脓疡性病证，现代可与肺脓肿、坏死性肺炎等部分病变比较，但不能仅凭症状确诊。喘咳、胸胁满是非特异性症状。木旺金衰属五行生克模型，不能理解为春季肝脏实际压迫肺脏。针灸学习上，若涉及肺系症状，常见相关经脉为手太阴肺经；但本段没有指定穴位，不能由病名直接推定取穴。

按︰《靈樞‧經筋篇》曰︰手心主之筋，其病當所過者支轉筋，前及胸痛息賁。此言手心主之筋，循脅腹，散胸中，下結於胃脘之賁門間，其病當筋之過接處為轉筋，而前及胸痛，散於胸中，結於賁門，故曰息賁。又曰︰太陰之筋，其病當所過者支轉筋，痛甚則成息賁，脅急吐血。此言手太陰之筋，散貫於賁門間，其病當筋之所過者為支度轉筋，而痛甚則成息賁，脅急吐血。蓋十二經筋合陰陽六氣，氣逆則為喘急息奔，血隨氣奔，則為吐血也。《素問‧陰陽別論》曰︰二陽之病發心脾，有不得隱曲，女子不月，其傳為風消，其傳為息賁者，死不治。此二陽者，足陽明胃，手陽明大腸也。病發於心脾者。其始必有得於隱曲之事，於是思則氣結，郁而為火，致損心營，心營既損，脾少生扶，則健運失職，飲食漸減，胃陰益虧；夫人身之精血，全賴後天穀氣榮養，今穀津日竭，鬱火內焚，是以男子少精，女子不月，血液日見乾枯，而大腸之傳道亦病，胃燥生火，火盛風生，則消爍肌肉，水精耗盡，金失其源，腎氣不納，逆傳於肺，致有喘息奔迫不治之証。此三者，似是而實非，不容不辨。奇病論帝曰︰病脅滿氣逆，二三歲不已，是為何病？岐伯曰︰病名息積，此不妨於食，不可灸刺，積為導引服藥，藥不能獨治也，此與本篇差同。藥難獨治，必兼導引之功，又不可不知也。

按语：《灵枢·经筋篇》说：手厥阴心包经的经筋，其病变发生在经筋循行经过的部位，可出现支撑不利、转筋，并向前引起胸痛和息贲。这里的意思是，手厥阴心包经经筋沿胁腹部循行，散布于胸中，向下连结到胃脘贲门附近。病变时，经筋经过或交接的部位会发生转筋，并可影响胸部而疼痛；经筋散布于胸中、连结贲门，所以称为息贲。又说：手太阴肺经的经筋，病变可在其循行部位出现支撑不利和转筋，疼痛严重时形成息贲，并出现胁部拘急、吐血。这里是说手太阴肺经经筋散布并贯通于贲门附近，经筋循行处可以发生牵掣、转筋，疼痛严重时则可能出现息贲、胁部拘急和吐血。作者认为，十二经筋与阴阳六气相合，气逆就会出现喘急、呼吸奔迫；血随气逆而上奔，就会出现吐血。《素问·阴阳别论》说：二阳的疾病发端于心脾，有隐曲方面的困难，女子会出现月经不来；病情传变可成为风消，也可成为息贲，后者预后极差。这里的二阳指足阳明胃经和手阳明大肠经。疾病发端于心脾，作者认为最初必然与隐曲之事有关，于是思虑导致气机郁结，郁久化火，损伤心营。心营受损后，脾失去滋养和支持，运化功能失常，饮食逐渐减少，胃阴也更加亏损。人体精血依靠后天水谷精微滋养，如今水谷津液逐渐耗竭，内部郁火持续消耗阴液，所以男子精少，女子月经停止，血液津液日益枯竭，大肠传导功能也发生障碍。胃燥生火，火盛生风，消耗肌肉；水液精气耗尽，肺金失去滋养来源，肾气不能纳气，病邪逆传于肺，于是出现严重而难治的喘息奔迫。这三种息贲相关论述表面相似，实际病机不同，不能不加以辨别。《素问·奇病论》中，黄帝问：胁部胀满、气机上逆，持续两三年不愈，这是什么病？岐伯回答：病名叫息积。这种病不妨碍饮食，不能使用艾灸和针刺治疗，应当通过导引和服药处理，单靠药物不能治愈。这与本篇的说法大致相同。药物难以单独治疗，必须配合导引，这一点也需要了解。

Commentary: The Lingshu, in the chapter 'Jing Jin', states that disorders of the sinew channel of Hand Jueyin may cause impaired support and movement, muscle spasms along its course, and anterior chest pain associated with Xi Ben. The explanation here is that the Hand Jueyin sinew channel follows the hypochondriac and abdominal regions, spreads through the chest, and descends to connect near the cardia of the Stomach. Its disorders may cause spasms where the sinews pass or join, with pain extending into the chest. Because the sinews spread through the chest and connect near the cardia, the condition is called Xi Ben. The text further states that disorders of the sinew channel of Taiyin may cause spasms along its course; severe pain may develop into Xi Ben, accompanied by hypochondriac tightness and vomiting of blood. This refers to the sinew channel of Hand Taiyin, which spreads and passes near the cardia. The author interprets spasms along its course and severe pain as potentially producing Xi Ben, hypochondriac tightness, and hematemesis. The twelve sinew channels correspond to Yin, Yang, and the six climatic influences. Rebellious Qi may cause urgent, labored breathing, and when blood follows the upward movement of Qi, vomiting of blood may occur. The Suwen, in the chapter 'Yinyang Bielun', states that a disorder of the two Yang channels originates in the Heart and Spleen, may involve difficulties related to private or sexual matters, and may cause amenorrhea in women. It may transform into Feng Xiao or Xi Ben; the latter is described as fatal and incurable. The two Yang channels here are the Foot Yangming Stomach channel and the Hand Yangming Large Intestine channel. The author explains that the disorder begins in the Heart and Spleen and is initially associated with concealed emotional or intimate concerns. Excessive thought causes Qi stagnation, which may transform into Fire and damage Heart nutritive Qi. Once Heart nutritive Qi is impaired, the Spleen loses support, its transportation and transformation functions deteriorate, food intake declines, and Stomach Yin becomes increasingly deficient. The body's Essence and Blood depend on nourishment from postnatal food and grain Qi. As fluids derived from food are depleted and internal Fire continues to consume them, men may develop reduced seminal Essence and women may develop amenorrhea. Blood and fluids become progressively depleted, and the Large Intestine's transportation function also becomes impaired. Dryness of the Stomach generates Fire; exuberant Fire generates Wind, consuming the flesh. As body fluids are exhausted, Lung Metal loses its source of nourishment, the Kidney fails to receive Qi, and the disorder reversely transmits to the Lung, causing severe, difficult-to-treat respiratory distress. These three descriptions may appear similar but actually represent different mechanisms and must be distinguished.
